Abstract: We derive and test kinematic upper bounds on charged energy extraction in the purely electric branch of a static $f(R,T)$ black hole family sourced by an Euler--Heisenberg type nonlinear electromagnetic sector. The analysis is restricted to external, minimally coupled probe particles on the fixed background. In this electric solution the field remains Coulombic up to an additive gauge constant; the nonlinear electromagnetic and matter-coupling coefficients therefore affect extraction indirectly through the metric, the event-horizon position, and the global causal structure. We obtain a general local bound retaining nonzero angular momentum and radial velocity of the negative energy fragment, and identify the commonly used zero angular momentum turning-point choice as an upper envelope rather than a generic decay configuration. A co-moving split supplies a locally four momentum-conserving benchmark. We classify horizons with the double root conditions $\mathcal F=\mathcal F'=0$, distinguish asymptotically flat, de Sitter, and anti-de Sitter branches, and impose the corresponding global outward-accessibility criterion. In the de Sitter static patch the electrostatic energy is referenced to the cosmological horizon, so the relevant scale is $Q(1/r_+-1/r_c)$ rather than $Q/r_+$. Reproducible scans compare Reissner--Nordström, Einstein--Euler--Heisenberg type, $f(R,T)$--Maxwell, and full $f(R,T)$--Euler--Heisenberg type backgrounds. The results show that the branch-referenced horizon potential controls the local upper envelope, whereas the asymptotic structure determines whether an outward trajectory can reach infinity, a cosmological horizon, or only a finite outer turning point.

</details>


### [100] [Constructing the canonical harmonic coordinates of Kerr metric to the fourth post-Minkowskian order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05713)
*Zizheng Xing,Xiaokai He,Zhoujian Cao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在MPM形式论框架下构造了Kerr度量的典范调和坐标至4PM阶，给出了无规范矩的精确典范MPM矩，计算了4PM典范度量，并证明其分量具有特定的奇偶性（对自旋参数a），与Jiang-Lin坐标进行对比展示其纯规范性质，同时与Damgaard等人的结果进行了交叉验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kerr度量的调和坐标表示在引力波物理和散射问题中具有重要意义，但已有的调和坐标（如Jiang-Lin坐标）含有非零的规范矩，缺乏"最纯净"的规范表示。作者旨在构造一个在MPM框架下完全无规范矩的Kerr度量的典范调和坐标，为后续研究提供更优的坐标基础。

Method: 基于Geroch-Hansen矩和Gürsel定理推导Kerr度量的精确典范MPM矩（无规范矩），然后从这些矩出发迭代计算gothic度规微扰至4PM阶，最终得到4PM典范度量。同时提取1PM阶Jiang-Lin坐标的源矩以作对比，并利用Schwarzschild情形全阶计算和Damgaard等人的动量空间递推结果进行交叉验证。

Result: 得到了Kerr度量在MPM框架下的4PM典范调和坐标表示；典范坐标的空间和时间分量是自旋参数a的偶函数，混合分量为奇函数；Jiang-Lin坐标从1PM阶即含有非零规范矩，而典范表示中规范矩全阶为零；Schwarzschild情形获得了全PM阶的闭合式结果；与Damgaard等人的4PM结果等价。

Conclusion: 典范调和坐标是Kerr度量在MPM框架下最"规范纯净"的表示，其奇偶性性质可作为区分不同调和坐标的标志。该工作为Kerr度量的后闵可夫斯基展开提供了更优的坐标基础，但连接典范坐标与已知调和坐标的坐标变换仍有待解决。

Abstract: In this paper we construct the canonical harmonic coordinates of the Kerr metric within the multipolar post-Minkowskian (MPM) formalism to the fourth post-Minkowskian (4PM) order. Based on the well known Geroch--Hansen moments of Kerr metric and Gürsel's theorem, we derive the exact canonical MPM moments $\mathrm{M}_L,\mathrm{S}_L$, which are free of any gauge moments. With these moments, we iteratively compute the gothic metric perturbation $h^{μν}_{\mathrm{can}}$ up to 4PM order and compute the 4PM canonical metric $g_{μν}^{\mathrm{can}}$. The resulting spatial and time components of these metrics are even functions of the spin parameter $a$ while the mixed components are odd. This parity property distinguishes the canonical coordinates from other harmonic coordinates. To contrast this minimal-gauge construction, we also extract the 1PM source moments of the Kerr metric in the Jiang--Lin coordinates. We find that the Jiang--Lin representation possesses non-vanishing gauge moments starting from the 1PM order, whereas in the canonical representation gauge moments vanish to all orders. This comparison highlights the canonical coordinates as the most gauge-pure representation of the Kerr metric in the MPM framework. The complete canonical metric for the Schwarzschild case is also computed to all PM orders. A recent independent construction by Damgaard et al. using momentum-space recursion yields 4PM equivalent results expressed as a power series in $a$, providing a cross-validation of our closed-form 4PM canonical metric. The coordinate transformation linking the canonical Kerr coordinates to previously known harmonic Kerr coordinates remains an open problem.

</details>


### [101] [How Loud Must a Neutron-Star Merger Be to Reveal Its Equation of State?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05794)
*Sk Md Adil Imam*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过贝叶斯嵌套抽样参数估计，研究了第三代引力波探测器（爱因斯坦望远镜加两台宇宙探索者）网络在双中子星并合过程中区分不同物态方程（EOS）所需的信噪比（SNR），发现证据差异遵循Δlog Z = A·SNR^n（n≈1.74-1.95）的普适标度律，并可据此预测所需的判别SNR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双中子星旋近过程中的潮汐响应编码了致密物质的物态方程信息，但尚未有系统框架量化第三代探测器区分竞争性EOS模型所需的信噪比，这对于规划探测器设计和观测策略至关重要。

Method: 对模拟的双中子星信号进行贝叶斯嵌套抽样参数估计，使用爱因斯坦望远镜加两台宇宙探索者探测器网络；比较正确与错误EOS恢复模型间的贝叶斯证据差，在广泛的SNR范围内扫描两种潮汐形变度对比、真值与恢复EOS互换、以及两个双星质量点，并通过Occam因子论证推导标度律。

Result: 发现证据差遵循Δlog Z = A·SNR^n的统一标度，指数n≈1.74-1.95；理论推导给出Δlog Z ∝ (ΔΛ̃·SNR)^2；利用三个配置校准该关系可在运行前以0.3%精度预测第四个配置下进行决定性EOS判别所需的SNR。

Conclusion: 建立了定量评估第三代引力波探测器网络EOS判别能力的理论框架，为运行前的观测规划和探测器性能预设提供了预测工具。

Abstract: The tidal response of neutron stars during binary inspiral encodes the equation of state (EOS) of dense matter in the gravitational-wave signal. Quantifying the signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) required to distinguish competing EOS models with third-generation detectors is therefore essential. We perform Bayesian nested-sampling parameter estimation on simulated binary neutron star signals observed by an Einstein Telescope plus two Cosmic Explorer detector network and compute the evidence difference between correct- and incorrect-EOS recovery models over a broad range of SNR. Across two tidal-deformability contrasts, a swap of the true and recovery EOS, and two binary mass points, we find a common scaling, $Δ\log Z = A\,\mathrm{SNR}^{n}$ with $n \simeq 1.74$--$1.95$, where the EOS contrast and binary properties determine only the prefactor $A$. This behavior follows from an Occam-factor argument, yielding $Δ\log Z \propto (Δ\tildeΛ\,\mathrm{SNR})^{2}$. Calibrating this relation on three configurations predicts, before the run, the SNR required for decisive EOS discrimination in the fourth to within $0.3\%$. These results establish a quantitative framework for assessing the EOS-discrimination reach of third-generation gravitational-wave detector networks.

</details>


### [102] [Asymmetric Quantum Oppenheimer-Snyder Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05818)
*Michał Bobula,Tomasz Pawłowski*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在圈量子宇宙学框架下研究均匀各向同性尘埃球的引力坍缩，发现了尘埃球表面出现物理激波，推导出两类类史瓦西真空线元，证明时空测地完备且因果结构丰富，首次在圈量子宇宙学一致框架中实现了完全动力学的正则黑洞形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典广义相对论中黑洞奇点不可避免，圈量子宇宙学有望通过量子几何效应消除奇点并产生正则黑洞，但此前缺乏在圈量子宇宙学一致框架下完全动力学的黑洞形成研究。

Method: 在Lemaitre-Tolman-Bondi时空中利用圈量子宇宙学环动力学（可约化为非对称圈量子宇宙学反弹场景），分析尘埃球经历幂律收缩后跟随类de Sitter膨胀的过程，并通过匹配内外部解来分析结构。

Result: 发现尘埃球表面出现物理激波（诱导度量连续但外曲率不连续），源于膨胀内部与收缩外部真空壳之间的动力学失配；推导出两类类史瓦西线元；证明所得时空测地完备，具有丰富因果结构，首次在圈量子宇宙学框架内实现完全动力学的正则黑洞形成。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在圈量子宇宙学一致框架下建立了完全动力学的正则黑洞形成机制，为量子引力时代黑洞物理提供了新的理论基础。

Abstract: We study black hole formation resulting from the collapse of a homogeneous and isotropic dust ball within a framework built upon loop quantum cosmology. Using loop dynamics formulated for Lemaitre-Tolman-Bondi spacetimes---which reduce to the asymmetric loop quantum cosmological bounce scenario---we analyze the dust ball undergoing power-law contraction followed by a de Sitter-like expansion. We discover an emergence of a physical shock at the dust ball surface, characterized by a continuous induced metric and a discontinuous extrinsic curvature arising from a dynamical mismatch between the expanding interior and the contracting exterior vacuum shells. Furthermore, we explicitly derive two variants of Schwarzschild-like line elements for the vacuum sector, and we demonstrate that the resulting spacetime is geodesically complete featuring a rich causal structure. This establishes, for the first time, fully dynamical regular black hole formation within a framework consistent with loop quantum cosmology.

</details>


### [103] [A Novel Kerr-like Black Hole in a General Double Power Law Dark Matter Environment: Geometry, Spectroscopy, and Energy Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05861)
*Supakchai Ponglertsakul,David Senjaya*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过Newman-Janis算法构造了嵌入双幂律暗物质环境中的Kerr-like黑洞解，并系统地分析了暗物质对旋转黑洞视界结构、极端自旋和曲率性质的影响。研究发现对于Dehnen型密度轮廓（γ≤2），旋转-晕轮相互作用可消除本质曲率奇点。进一步通过解析低频匹配方法研究了Dehnen (1,4,γ)暗物质晕中大规模标量扰动，推导出准束缚态谱、标量云形成条件及超辐射放大因子，并揭示了热标量场旋转能量提取效率受暗物质参数控制的规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究大多关注暗物质对静态黑洞的影响，而旋转黑洞嵌入真实暗物质环境（如双幂律密度分布）的统一框架仍然缺失。需要构建一个通用的旋转时空解，以理解暗物质晕与黑洞旋转之间的耦合效应如何改变黑洞的基本性质（视界、奇点）以及其观测特征（光谱、能量提取）。

Method: 采用Newman-Janis算法将Schwarzschild-like种子度规旋转化，构造嵌入一般双幂律暗物质密度分布的统一旋转时空框架。对于标量扰动分析，采用解析低频匹配方法推导Dehnen (1,4,γ)暗物质晕中大规模标量场的准束缚态谱、标量云形成阈值和超辐射放大因子，并分析热标量场的旋转能量提取效率。

Result: (1) 对于Dehnen型密度轮廓（γ≤2），旋转与暗物质晕的相互作用可消除静态配置中存在的本质曲率奇点；(2) 暗物质参数ρ₀r₀³和γ在标量谱上留下特征印记：增加晕密度或尖度会增强准束缚态的束缚并加快其衰变，移动标量云阈值，并通过收窄允许频率窗口和降低放大因子峰值来抑制超辐射放大；(3) 旋转能量提取效率由热谱与超辐射不稳定性之间的相互作用控制，较低的温度和较不尖锐的密度轮廓产生更高效的能量提取。

Conclusion: 本文建立了旋转黑洞与双幂律暗物质环境相互作用的统一理论框架，揭示了暗物质晕不仅显著改变黑洞的视界结构和奇点性质，还为标量谱和能量提取机制留下独特的可观测特征。这些结果对未来将暗物质环境影响纳入黑洞天体物理观测解释具有重要意义。

Abstract: We construct a novel Kerr-like black hole solution embedded in a general double power law dark matter environment by applying the Newman--Janis algorithm to a Schwarzschild-like seed geometry. This framework provides a unified rotating spacetime for arbitrary double power law density profiles and reveals how dark matter modifies the horizon structure, extremal spin, and curvature properties of rotating black holes. Remarkably, we find that the rotation--halo interplay can eliminate essential curvature singularities for Dehnen-type profiles with $γ\leq2$, despite the singular nature of the corresponding static configurations. We then investigate the spectroscopic signatures of the dark matter environment through massive scalar perturbations in the Dehnen $(1,4,γ)$ halo. Using an analytical low-frequency matching method, we derive the quasibound state spectrum, the onset condition for scalar cloud formation, and superradiant amplification factor, showing that the halo parameters $ρ_0 r_0^3$ and $γ$ leave characteristic imprints on the scalar spectrum. Increasing the halo density or the cusp strengthens the binding of quasibound states and enhances their decay, shifts the scalar cloud threshold, and suppresses superradiant amplification effectivity by narrowing the allowed frequency window and lowering the amplification factor peak. Finally, we analyze rotational energy extraction from thermal scalar fields and demonstrate that the efficiency is controlled by the interplay between the thermal spectrum and the superradiant instability, with lower temperatures and less cuspy density profiles yielding more efficient energy extraction.

</details>


### [104] [A Scale-Invariant Theory of the Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05929)
*Julian Barbour,Maria I. R. Lourenço*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨了如何通过采用莱布尼茨充分理由原则引导的关系本体论，消去牛顿引入的绝对位置、方向、时间和尺度等不可观测结构，从而将引力N体问题重新表述为仅依赖无量纲比率的尺度不变形式。该框架以尺度不变流形V为核心，提供了结构度量、形状排序和涌现的引力时间箭头，统一了此前被认为不同的牛顿解类别，并可能揭示新的对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代物理学在取得巨大经验成功的同时，保留了牛顿引入的大量绝对、不可观测的结构，而这些结构的必要性从未被充分质疑。作者希望基于莱布尼茨的充分理由原则，探讨这些绝对结构能否被消除，从而建立更经济、更基础的本体论。

Method: 依次消去绝对位置、方向、时间和尺度，将引力N体问题重新表述为仅涉及无量纲比率的尺度不变形式；以尺度不变流形V作为核心数学对象，在其中定义结构度量、形状排序和引力时间箭头。

Result: 该框架统一了此前被视作不同的牛顿解类别，可能发现了新的对称性；提供了一种基于无时间空间关联而非时间演化的解释概念，指向更经济的本体论，并可能为广义相对论和量子力学提供新视角。

Conclusion: 尽管在牛顿引力框架下发展，但所提出的原理可能为广义相对论和量子力学提供新的视角，暗示一种更为经济、减少不必要绝对结构的基础物理学表述方式。

Abstract: Modern physics has achieved extraordinary empirical success while retaining much of the absolute, unobservable structure introduced by Newton, largely without questioning its necessity. We investigate how far this structure can be eliminated by adopting a relational ontology guided by Leibniz's principle of sufficient reason. Removing absolute position, orientation, time and, finally, scale leads naturally to a formulation of the gravitational $N$-body problem where only dimensionless ratios are physically meaningful. Within this framework, the scale-invariant variety $V$ becomes a central quantity, providing a measure of structure, a natural ordering of shapes, and an emergent gravitational arrow of time. We argue that the resulting formulation unifies classes of Newtonian solutions previously regarded as distinct, uncovering a possible new symmetry, suggests a notion of explanation based on timeless spatial correlations rather than temporal evolution, and points towards a more economical ontology. Although developed in the context of Newtonian gravity, the principles proposed here may also offer a new perspective on general relativity and quantum mechanics.

</details>


### [105] [Coherent End-to-End Search for Generic Extreme-Mass-Ratio Inspirals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05974)
*Xiaobo Zou,Xingyu Zhong,Wen-Biao Han,Soumya D. Mohanty*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自适应收缩搜索空间的相干分层策略，首次实现了对具有天体物理宽先验的通用极端质量比旋进（EMRI）信号的端到端相干参数估计，在LISA噪声下获得了高拟合因子和精确的参数恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EMRI信号包含超过10^5个强场轨道周期，是空间引力波干涉仪的关键目标，但此前在宽先验下对通用系统进行相干恢复的问题尚未解决。历届Mock LISA、LISA和Taiji数据挑战均未产生完全可靠的通解。核心障碍在于针尖捞针式的似然函数：六个相位演化参数张成巨大参数域，全局最大值极窄且被大量次极大值包围。

Method: 论文发现高似然次极大值逐渐向全局最大值集中，可引导搜索体积的自适应收缩。据此采用降维剖面似然（reduced-dimensional profile likelihood）和相干分层搜索策略，覆盖完整14维参数空间。

Result: 在平稳高斯LISA噪声下，该方法恢复两个半年时长的解析-克鲁格（analytical-kludge）信号（信噪比约50），拟合因子分别达到0.989和0.971，相位演化参数分数误差为10^-3至10^-2，距离误差约3%，天区定位误差小于0.1弧度。

Conclusion: 该方法将似然次极大值转化为相干分层搜索的引导信息，首次实现了在宽天体物理先验下对通用EMRI信号的端到端相干参数估计，为未来空间引力波任务中的盲检测提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Extreme-mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) encode more than $10^5$ strong-field orbital cycles and are key targets for space-borne gravitational-wave interferometers, yet coherent recovery of generic systems over astrophysically broad priors remains unresolved. Successive Mock LISA, LISA, and Taiji Data Challenges (MLDCs, LDCs, and TDCs) have not yet produced a complete, generally reliable solution for blind EMRI detection and parameter recovery across such priors. The central obstacle is a needle-in-a-haystack likelihood: six phase-evolution parameters span a vast domain, producing an exceptionally narrow global maximum amid numerous secondary maxima. We show that higher-likelihood secondary maxima concentrate progressively around the global maximum and can therefore guide an adaptive contraction of the search volume. We exploit this structure through a reduced-dimensional profile likelihood and a coherent hierarchical strategy to search for EMRI signals across the full 14-dimensional parameter space. This enables the first end-to-end coherent parameter estimation for generic EMRIs with astrophysically broad priors. In stationary Gaussian LISA noise, the search recovers two half-year analytical-kludge signals with signal-to-noise ratios near 50, yielding fitting factors of 0.989 and 0.971, fractional errors of $10^{-3}$--$10^{-2}$ in the phase-evolution parameters and near $3\%$ in the distance, and error of less than $0.1$ radian in the sky location. The method turns secondary maxima into guides for a coherent hierarchical search.

</details>


### [106] [Radial spectra and dynamical signatures of excited boson stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06067)
*Chen-Hao Hao,Wen-Di Guo,Qin Tan,Jieci Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文针对球对称玻色子星（含mini玻色子星和四次自相互作用模型），沿具有固定径向节点数的平衡分支计算其最低径向模式。通过将脉动方程重构为在背景标量场零点处保持正则的加性变量，特征值问题得以直接穿越激发构型的节点进行积分。所有分支中，约束基态径向特征值的首个零点与ADM质量、Noether电荷和束缚能的首个同时临界点在数值精度内一致。文章还评估了非线性球对称演化中识别出的阈值模型的径向特征值，并发现了与节点数和自相互作用强度的简单经验关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激发态玻色子星的线性稳定性缺乏正则的扰动框架，现有方法在背景标量场零点处存在奇异性，难以直接积分脉动方程；此外，约束径向模式、平衡临界点与非线性稳定性诊断之间的关系尚不明确。

Method: (1) 将脉动方程改写在加性变量（additive variables）中，确保在背景标量场零点处保持正则；(2) 沿固定节点数的平衡分支直接积分特征值问题，计算最低径向模式；(3) 对mini玻色子星和四次自相互作用模型分别求解；(4) 将特征值零点与平衡临界点（ADM质量、Noether电荷、束缚能）进行对比；(5) 对非线性演化中识别的阈值模型进行径向特征值评估并拟合经验关系。

Result: (1) 正则重整化方法使得特征值问题能够穿越激发构型的节点进行积分；(2) 约束基态径向特征值的首零点与ADM质量、Noether电荷、束缚能的首个同时临界点在数值精度内重合；(3) 阈值模型的径向特征值随节点数和自相互作用强度呈现简单的经验关联。

Conclusion: 该工作为激发态玻色子星提供了正则的线性扰动框架，阐明约束径向模式、平衡临界点与非线性稳定性诊断之间的对应关系，为激发态玻色子星的稳定性判定提供了可靠依据。

Abstract: We compute the lowest radial mode of spherically symmetric boson stars along equilibrium branches with a fixed number of radial nodes, considering both mini boson stars and quartically self-interacting models. By reformulating the pulsation equations in additive variables that remain regular at the zeros of the background scalar field, the eigenvalue problem can be integrated directly through the nodes of excited configurations. For all branches examined, the first zero of the constrained fundamental radial eigenvalue coincides, within numerical resolution, with the first simultaneous critical point of the Arnowitt--Deser--Misner (ADM) mass, Noether charge, and binding energy. We further evaluate the radial eigenvalue for the threshold models identified in nonlinear spherical evolutions of excited boson stars and find a simple empirical correlation with the node number and self-interaction strength. Our results provide a regular perturbative framework for excited boson stars and clarify the relation between constrained radial modes, equilibrium critical points, and nonlinear stability diagnostics.

</details>


### [107] [Quasinormal Modes of Gauss--Bonnet Black Holes via the Spectral Method: Scalar, Vector, and Tensor Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06083)
*Davide Batic,Denys Dutykh*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文使用高精度Chebyshev谱方法，系统地研究了D维（5,6,7,8,10,11,12,26）Schwarzschild黑洞在Gauss-Bonnet修正下的标量、矢量和张量拟正则模（QNMs），发现了过阻尼纯虚模态、高泛音实部非单调行为、维度增强效应等鲁棒特征。在标量和矢量扇区发现了精确的等谱性并给出基于Darboux分解的解析证明；在张量扇区首次数值确认了六维不稳定性，其阈值满足Cohn-Calogero界且质量阈值$GM\leq 158.1\alpha^{3/2}$。高维增强模态可能进入DECIGO等未来空间探测器的灵敏度窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet引力中黑洞拟正则模的精确计算在强耦合和高泛音区域超出了WKB与特征积分等标准近似方法的可靠性范围，需要更高精度的数值手段来系统研究不同自旋扇区QNM谱的高曲率动力学特征、等谱性与不稳定性。

Method: 采用高精度Chebyshev谱方法对D维（5-26维）Gauss-Bonnet修正Schwarzschild黑洞的标量、矢量、张量三个自旋扇区的QNM谱进行计算，并与六阶WKB和特征积分方法进行对比验证。对于等谱性结论，利用相应的Hamiltonian的Darboux因式分解给出解析证明。

Result: 发现了三类鲁棒的高曲率特征：过阻尼纯虚模态的出现、高泛音实部的非单调行为、弦理论动机维度下无量纲QNM频率的强烈放大。在标量单极子与矢量偶极子之间发现了精确等谱性并提供了解析证明；首次数值确认了六维张量扇区的不稳定性，onset由Cohn-Calogero界精确刻画，质量阈值$GM\leq 158.1\alpha^{3/2}$，而D≥7时无类似不稳定性也无张量等谱性。

Conclusion: 该研究为Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet引力中的QNMs提供了全面基准，突显了标准近似方案在强耦合和高泛音区域的局限性。高维增强模态的物理频率可能落入DECIGO等未来天基探测器的灵敏度窗口，为弦理论启发的引力修正提供了可检验的引力波预言。

Abstract: We present a unified study of scalar, vector, and tensor quasinormal modes (QNMs) of Schwarzschild black holes corrected by a Gauss--Bonnet (GB) term in higher dimensions. Using a high-precision Chebyshev spectral method, we map the QNM spectra across $D\in\{5,6,7,8,10,11,12,26\}$ well beyond the regime where sixth-order WKB and characteristic-integration techniques remain reliable. Across the three spin sectors, we find several robust signatures of higher-curvature dynamics: the appearance of overdamped purely imaginary modes, non-monotonic behaviour in the real parts of higher overtones, and a strong amplification of the dimensionless QNM frequencies in string-motivated dimensions. In the scalar and vector sectors, we uncover an exact isospectrality between the scalar monopole ($\ell=0$) and vector dipole ($\ell=1$) at vanishing GB coupling, and we provide an analytic proof based on a Darboux factorisation of the corresponding Hamiltonians. In the tensor sector, we obtain the first numerical confirmation of the long-predicted instability in six dimensions; its onset is sharply captured by the Cohn--Calogero bound and leads to the mass threshold $GM\leq 158.1\,α^{3/2}$. No analogous instability is found for $D\geq 7$, and no tensor isospectrality occurs. Converting the dimensionless frequencies to physical units suggests that the amplified modes in higher dimensions may enter the sensitivity window of future space-based detectors such as DECIGO. The merged analysis provides a comprehensive benchmark for QNMs in Einstein--Gauss--Bonnet gravity and highlights the limitations of standard approximation schemes in the strong-coupling and high-overtone regimes.

</details>


### [108] [Spinning Particle Dynamics and Observational Redshift around an Asymptotically Flat Symmergent Black Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06114)
*Beyhan Puliçe,Ali Övgün*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究渐近平坦对称引力（symmergent gravity）中微扰可变标量曲率分支下的类时粒子动力学、碰撞能量学与光子频移。线性阶外部几何通过满足线性方程的径向模式共形于史瓦西时空，对称参数γ的两种符号产生不同变形（γ>0为汤川衰减，γ<0为振荡型反比衰减）。作者推导了中性、带电和自旋粒子的径向方程、有效势及圆轨道条件，并计算了质心碰撞能量和光子红/蓝移因子，发现两者满足(1+z+)(1+z-)=1/A(r_e)这一与度规函数直接关联的乘积关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称引力理论提供了在无引入新自由度前提下从量子场论玻色-费米不平衡生成广义相对论修正的框架，其中R²修正系数由底层量子场论决定。然而该理论中可变标量曲率分支的粒子动力学与观测效应尚未被系统研究，作者旨在通过分析粒子轨道、碰撞能量和光子频移等可观测物理量，为探测这一可变曲率扇区提供理论预言。

Method: 在渐近平坦对称引力框架下，取低能真空作用量含R²修正（系数由玻色-费米不平衡设定），在线性阶将外部几何构造为共形于史瓦西时空的形式。对中性粒子求解测地线方程，对带电粒子采用试探场近似，对自旋粒子采用Mathisson-Papapetrou-Dixon方程并施加Tulczyjew条件，分别推导径向方程、有效势和圆轨道稳定性条件。进一步计算中性粒子碰撞的质心能量，以及由圆轨道测地源切向发射、被无穷远静态观测者接收的光子频移因子。

Result: 推导出完整的中性/带电/自旋粒子径向方程与轨道条件；发现γ>0分支给出平滑短程史瓦西偏离，γ<0分支产生振荡径向带并允许独立检验稳定性的圆轨道解。红移和蓝移因子满足(1+z+)(1+z-)=1/A(r_e)，将乘积直接与发射处的度规函数lapse关联。观测量的定量解释依赖变形振幅及振荡分支的相位。

Conclusion: 本文建立的可观测量（轨道动力学、碰撞能量、光子频移）为探测可变曲率扇区提供了互补的检验手段。上下频移因子的乘积关系是一个理论精确关系，可作为该模型的标志性预言。然而，定量可检验性受制于变形振幅与相位的不确定性，需结合独立约束来增强预测能力。

Abstract: We investigate timelike particle dynamics, collision energetics, and photon frequency shifts in the perturbative variable-scalar curvature branch of asymptotically flat symmergent gravity. The low-energy vacuum action contains an $R^{2}$ correction whose coefficient is set by the boson--fermion imbalance of the underlying quantum field theory. At linear order, the exterior geometry is conformal to Schwarzschild spacetime through a radial mode satisfying a linear equation. We retain an independent boundary-condition-dependent amplitude and restrict the analysis to the perturbative domain. The two signs of the symmergent parameter $γ$ yield distinct profiles: $γ>0$ gives a Yukawa-suppressed deformation, whereas $γ<0$ produces an oscillatory inverse-radius deformation. We derive radial equations, effective potentials, circular-orbit and marginal-stability conditions for neutral, electrically charged, and spinning massive particles. Charged particles are treated in the test-field approximation, while spinning particles obey the Mathisson--Papapetrou--Dixon equations with the Tulczyjew condition. We also compute the center-of-mass energy of neutral-particle collisions and the frequency shifts of photons emitted tangentially by circular geodesic sources and detected by a static observer at infinity. The redshift and blueshift factors satisfy $(1+z_{+})(1+z_{-})=1/A(r_e)$, directly linking their product to the lapse function at emission. The $γ>0$ branch yields smooth, short-range deviations from Schwarzschild dynamics, whereas the $γ<0$ branch can generate oscillatory radial bands admitting circular-orbit solutions whose stability must be tested independently. These observables provide complementary probes of the variable-curvature sector, although their quantitative interpretation also depends on the deformation amplitude and, for the oscillatory branch, its phase.

</details>


### [109] [A Neutron Star Hidden Inside a Black Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06224)
*Chen Tan,Yong-Qiang Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究暗物质吸附中子星（dark matter admixed neutron stars），其中中子星与由Einasto密度分布描述的各向异性暗物质晕共存。通过求解两种物态方程（BSk19和SLy4）下的修正Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff方程，发现暗物质晕显著改变中子星结构，且在特定暗物质晕参数范围内，$g_{rr}^{-1}$在星体表面外变号形成事件视界，出现"黑洞中的中子星"这一奇特构型，且该构型与物态方程的具体选择无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索中子星与暗物质晕共存时的结构变化，并为"黑洞内部究竟是什么"这一基本问题提供一个新的、可具体计算的实例视角。此前已有工作表明Einasto密度分布可产生正则无奇异黑洞解，本文将其应用于中子星-暗物质共存系统。

Method: 采用Einasto密度分布描述各向异性暗物质晕，将其与中子星物质一起纳入修正的Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff方程框架；使用两种不同的物态方程（BSk19和SLy4）进行数值求解，系统扫描暗物质晕参数空间，考察$g_{rr}^{-1}$的符号变化以判断事件视界是否形成。

Result: 暗物质晕对中子星结构产生显著影响；在特定暗物质晕参数范围内，$g_{rr}^{-1}$在中子星表面之外变号，形成事件视界，中子星作为内部正则配置持续存在，即"黑洞中的中子星"构型。该构型在BSk19和SLy4两种物态方程中均出现，不依赖于物态方程的具体选择。

Conclusion: "黑洞中的中子星"这一构型的发现为研究黑洞内部结构提供了新的视角和具体的可计算实例，且该构型具有物态方程无关的普适性。

Abstract: We investigate dark matter admixed neutron stars in which the neutron star coexists with an anisotropic dark matter halo described by the Einasto density profile, a model recently shown to produce regular, singularity-free black hole solutions~[Phys. Rev. D \textbf{113}, 043011 (2026)]. Solving the modified Tolman--Oppenheimer--Volkoff equations with two different equations of state (BSk19 and SLy4), we find that the dark matter halo significantly alters the neutron star structure. Furthermore, for a specific range of halo parameters, $g_{rr}^{-1}$ changes sign outside the stellar surface, forming an event horizon with the neutron star persisting as a regular configuration inside it--- ``neutron stars in black holes''. This configuration appears in both equations of state and does not depend on a specific choice of the equation of state. The discovery of this configuration provides a new perspective and a concrete computable instance for the study of what lies inside a black hole.

</details>

### [110] [Classification of some $\mathbb{Z}/2\mathbb{Z}\times \mathbb{Z}/2\mathbb{Z}$-quadratic fusion categories of rank 6](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06064)
*Yue Meng,Zhiqiang Yu*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文对秩为6且为Z/2Z×Z/2Z-二次型的融合范畴进行了部分分类：当融合规则重数小于20时，其Grothendieck环必为九个融合环之一；其中五个的分类化此前已知，并证明剩余四个中有一个可通过Z/2Z×Z/4Z+8型近群融合范畴的去等变实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 融合范畴的分类是张量范畴理论中的核心问题，而具有二次群结构（G(C)同构于Klein四元群且其余简单对象构成单一轨道）的融合范畴具有特殊的重要性。作者旨在对秩为6的这类范畴进行部分分类，以推进此类范畴的完整分类工作。

Method: 作者利用Grothendieck环的结构分析，结合融合规则重数小于20的假设，枚举出所有可能的融合环；然后通过去等变（de-equivariantization）技术，将已知的Z/2Z×Z/4Z+8型近群融合范畴转化为目标融合环，从而构造实现。

Result: 证明了在融合规则重数小于20的条件下，秩为6的Z/2Z×Z/2Z-二次融合范畴的Grothendieck环只能取九个融合环之一；其中五个的分类化已被先前文献所知，另有一个可通过近群融合范畴的去等变实现。

Conclusion: 该结果为Z/2Z×Z/2Z-二次融合范畴的部分分类提供了新进展，缩小了待分类的候选融合环的范围，并提供了新的实现构造。剩余三个融合环的分类化问题仍有待解决。

Abstract: A fusion category $\mathcal{C}$ is said to be $\mathbb{Z}/2\mathbb{Z}\times \mathbb{Z}/2\mathbb{Z}$-quadratic if the group $G(\mathcal{C})$ of invertible objects is isomorphic to $\mathbb{Z}/2\mathbb{Z}\times \mathbb{Z}/2\mathbb{Z}$, and the remaining simple objects form an orbit under the action of $G(\mathcal{C})$. In this paper, we give a partial classification of $\mathbb{Z}/2\mathbb{Z}\times \mathbb{Z}/2\mathbb{Z}$-quadratic fusion categories of rank six. More precisely, we show that its Grothendieck ring $\mathcal{K}_0(\mathcal{C})$ must be one of nine fusion rings if the fusion rule multiplicities are less than $20$, and the categorifications of five of them are previously known. We prove that one of the last four fusion rings can be realized as de-equivariantization of a near-group fusion category of type $\mathbb{Z}/2\mathbb{Z}\times \mathbb{Z}/4\mathbb{Z}+8$.

</details>


### [111] [Relative modular categories from $\mathfrak{osp}(2 \vert 2n)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06333)
*Seth Porter,Matthew B. Young*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 该论文通过研究正交辛李超代数 𝔬𝔰𝔭(2|2n) 的展开量子群的加权表示理论，构造了相对模范畴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相对模范畴是模范畴的推广，在量子拓扑和不变量理论中具有重要应用。作者希望从正交辛李超代数的量子群出发，建立相应的相对模范畴，从而扩展模范畴的构造框架。

Method: 利用展开量子群（unrolled quantum group）的加权表示理论，研究 𝔬𝔰𝔭(2|2n) 的表示范畴结构，并通过构造适当的 Hopf 代数结构及其模范畴，建立相对模范畴。

Result: 成功从 𝔬𝔰𝔭(2|2n) 的展开量子群的加权表示理论中构造出相对模范畴。

Conclusion: 该工作表明正交辛李超代数的展开量子群可以作为构造相对模范畴的有效工具，为后续研究量子不变量和拓扑应用奠定基础。

Abstract: We construct relative modular categories from the weighted representation theory of the unrolled quantum group of the orthosymplectic Lie superalgebra $\mathfrak{osp}(2 \vert 2n)$.

</details>

### [112] [Tree-Level Factorization Obstruction in Monopole Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05272)
*Hsing-Yi Lai,John Terning*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明，从带电粒子对产生磁单极子-反磁单极子对的树级振幅无法仅通过最小单光子耦合构造。电和磁三点顶点的胶合虽然给出非零光子极点贡献，但所得到的磁末态与电初态具有相反的离散对称性本征值。局域四点相互作用无法改变由三点耦合固定的极点贡献，因此无法修复这一不匹配。对于费米子，该阻碍特定于最小三点耦合：独立的Pauli形状因子提供自旋单态三点振幅，从而满足对称性选择规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索磁单极子对产生的树级振幅结构，研究其在离散对称性约束下能否由最小单光子耦合构成的电-磁三点顶点胶合而成，并考察局域四点相互作用或附加形状因子是否能修复对称性失配问题。

Method: 通过胶合电、磁三点顶点构造树级四粒子振幅，检查所得磁末态的离散对称性本征值是否与电初态一致；分析局域四点相互作用对光子极点贡献的影响；对费米子情形，引入独立的Pauli形状因子并构造自旋单态三点振幅以检验对称性选择规则。

Result: 最小单光子耦合构造的电-磁三点顶点胶合会产生非零光子极点贡献，但离散对称性本征值在初末态之间发生翻转，无法匹配；局域四点相互作用不能修正这一固定的极点贡献。然而对于费米子，引入独立的Pauli形状因子可以构造自旋单态三点振幅，从而满足对称性选择规则。

Conclusion: 磁单极子对产生振幅的构造中存在与最小单光子耦合相关的离散对称性阻碍，该阻碍源于三点耦合的固定极点结构；Pauli形状因子的引入为费米子情形提供了一条绕过该阻碍的途径。

Abstract: We show that the tree-level amplitude for producing a monopole--antimonopole pair from an electrically charged pair cannot be constructed from the minimal one-photon couplings. Gluing the electric and magnetic three-point vertices gives a nonzero photon-pole contribution, but the resulting magnetic final state has the opposite discrete-symmetry eigenvalue from the electric initial state. Local four-point interactions cannot change the pole contribution fixed by the three-point couplings, so they cannot repair this mismatch. For fermions, the obstruction is specific to the minimal three-point coupling: an independent Pauli form factor supplies a spin-singlet three-point amplitude and allows the symmetry selection rule to be satisfied.

</details>


### [113] [Exact Lattice Identities and Continuum-Limit Dyson--Schwinger Equations for Yang-Mills Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05415)
*Arpan Chatterjee,Marco Frasca,Anish Ghoshal,Stefan Groote*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从SU(N)格点规范理论出发，直接基于链接变量$U_\mu(m)\in SU(N)$和Haar测度的左平移不变性，严格推导了连续极限下的Dyson–Schwinger方程，避免了以李代数取值规范势作为有限格距下基本积分变量所带来的歧义，并进一步通过Feynman规范简化得到一、两点连通函数的方程，建立了格点精确恒等式与连续非微扰泛函方程之间的直接桥梁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者试图为格点Yang–Mills理论的Dyson–Schwinger方程提供严格的格点推导，以弥合格点精确恒等式与连续统非微扰泛函方程之间的鸿沟。传统方法在有限格距下引入李代数取值的规范势作为积分变量存在内在歧义，而直接在紧致规范群上构造可以规避这一问题。

Method: 利用SU(N)上Haar测度在左群平移下的不变性，直接在链接变量$U_\mu(m)$上构造Dyson–Schwinger恒等式，并通过左不变李导数作用于单个链接来表达Wilson作用量对应的主方程。接着在第二部分通过选取Feynman规范来分解规范自由度，分析格点恒等式的连续极限形式，推导一、两点连通函数的方程，并在进一步简化下使其闭合为可解的标量系统。

Result: 得到了格点Yang–Mills理论Dyson–Schwinger方程在连续极限下的严格推导形式；在Feynman规范下导出了一、两点连通函数的方程，并在约化条件下闭合为标量系统。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了格点精确Dyson–Schwinger恒等式与连续非微扰Yang–Mills研究中常用泛函方程之间的直接桥梁，为两种框架的交叉验证和相互补充提供了严格的数学基础。

Abstract: Starting from SU(N) on the lattice, we give a rigorous derivation of the Dyson--Schwinger equations in the continuum limit. We formulate the Dyson--Schwinger identities for the lattice Yang--Mills theory directly in terms of the link variables $U_μ(m)\in SU(N)$, exploiting the invariance of the Haar measure under left group translations. This provides an exact lattice derivation of the corresponding master equation for the Wilson action, expressed through left-invariant Lie derivatives acting on individual links. Because the construction is carried out directly on the compact gauge group, it avoids the ambiguities associated with introducing Lie-algebra valued gauge potentials as primary integration variables at finite lattice spacing. For practical applications, in a second part we then break down the gauge degree of freedom by choosing Feynman gauge. We analyze the continuum-limit form of the resulting lattice identities and derive equations for the one- and two-point connected functions. Under a further simplifying reduction, these equations close to a tractable scalar system. Our results establish a direct bridge between exact lattice identities and the functional equations commonly used in continuum nonperturbative studies of Yang--Mills theory.

</details>


### [114] [Moduli Bounds from Spin-2 Sum Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05273)
*Francesco Bertucci,James Bonifacio,Kurt Hinterbichler*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用共形自举（conformal bootstrap）方法，证明了Mirbabayi和Villadoro提出的万有上界：在引力Kaluza-Klein（KK）理论中，最轻的KK引力子（质量m₁）必须耦合到至少一个标量场，该标量质量m_sc满足(m_sc/m₁)² ≤ 4/3。此外，文中还证明每个更重的KK引力子（质量m_n）必须耦合到满足(m_sc/m_n)² < 36/25的标量场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mirbabayi和Villadoro通过数值证据提出了一个关于引力Kaluza-Klein理论中质量最轻标量场质量上界的猜想，但缺乏严格的解析证明。本文旨在通过共形自举方法给出该上界的严格证明，从而为KK理论中引力子与标量场的耦合关系提供更坚实的理论基础。

Method: 本文采用共形自举（conformal bootstrap）方法，将其应用于大质量自旋-2散射振幅的求和规则（sum rules）。通过对这些求和规则进行分析，推导出KK理论中引力子与标量场耦合所必须满足的约束条件，从而证明质量上界。

Result: 成功证明了最轻KK引力子（质量m₁）必须耦合到至少一个质量满足(m_sc/m₁)² ≤ 4/3的标量场。此外还证明了每个更重的KK引力子（质量m_n）必须耦合到至少一个满足(m_sc/m_n)² < 36/25的标量场。

Conclusion: 该研究为Mirbabayi-Villadoro猜想的万有上界提供了严格证明，并通过共形自举方法进一步推广到所有更重的KK引力子模态，深化了对引力KK理论中低能标量场存在的必然性的理解。

Abstract: Mirbabayi and Villadoro have provided strong numerical evidence for a universal upper bound on the mass of the lightest scalar field in gravitational Kaluza--Klein (KK) theories. This bound states that the lightest KK graviton, with mass $m_{1}$, must couple to at least one scalar with mass $m_{\rm sc}$ such that $(m_{\rm sc} /m_{1})^2 \leq 4/3$. We give a proof of this bound using the approach of the conformal bootstrap applied to sum rules for massive spin-2 scattering amplitudes. We additionally show that each heavier KK graviton, with mass $m_n$, must couple to at least one scalar with mass $m_{\rm sc}$ such that $(m_{\rm sc} /m_{n})^2 < 36/25$.

</details>


### [115] [Adiabatic Deformations of Black Hole Moduli: I. Canonical Slices and Fredholm Solvability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05275)
*Karim Benakli,Anna Chrysostomou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究静态黑洞在缓慢演化渐近模数下的绝热响应问题，将问题分解为几何与泛函分析两部分，分别用显式标量判据刻画，最终将解的存在唯一性归结为单个标量匹配条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解静态黑洞如何响应外部缓慢变化的背景场（由渐近模数参数化），并发展一种框架，将绝热响应问题从几何流形选择与泛函分析求解两个层面分别刻画，从而获得显式可判定的条件。

Method: 通过微分光滑静态解族得到切空间上的内协向量，其核标识典范切线方向；将静态族提升为缓慢演化瞬时代表后，不满足精确场方程，所得差异定义滞后场，由约化径向算子控制。通过表示约定分离精确动力学视界与瞬时代表视界，再通过局部切片移除切向歧义，诱导约化算子的有限秩摄动。利用Fredholm指标保持性分析核的位移，在横截性与Fredholm假设下将边界值问题归结为标量匹配条件。

Result: 建立了两个显式标量判据分别刻画几何切线方向识别与泛函解的存在唯一性；证明了在横截性与Fredholm假设下，完整的一阶绝热边界值问题的存在唯一性等价于单个标量匹配条件；有限秩摄动非平凡时，典范切线零模从约化算子核中移出。

Conclusion: 该框架仅依赖光滑非极值静态解流形与约化算子的Fredholm性质，为绝热黑洞响应问题提供了统一且可显式判定的构造方案，并引出针对磁性GHS族的专门研究。

Abstract: The leading adiabatic response of a static black hole to a slowly evolving asymptotic modulus separates into a geometric problem and a functional-analytic one: the former identifies the tangent direction on the manifold of static solutions actually followed by the black hole, and the latter determines whether the resulting deformation admits a unique solution. We show that both can be characterized by explicit scalar criteria. Differentiating a smooth family of static solutions defines an intrinsic covector on its tangent space. When the family is parameterized by the asymptotic modulus and the static horizon-mass parameter, the kernel of this covector identifies a canonical tangent direction that coincides with the one selected by a slowly varying exterior modulus. Promoting the static family to a slowly evolving instantaneous representative does not solve the exact field equations; the resulting discrepancy defines a lag field governed, after elimination of the metric perturbations, by a single reduced radial operator. A representation convention separates the exact dynamical horizon from that of the instantaneous representative. A local slice then removes the remaining tangential ambiguity and induces a finite-rank perturbation of the reduced operator. The Fredholm index is preserved, and, whenever the finite-rank update is nontrivial, the canonical tangent zero mode is displaced from the kernel of the reduced operator. Under the transversality and Fredholm hypotheses, existence and uniqueness of the completed leading-order adiabatic boundary-value problem reduce to a single scalar matching condition on the resulting kernel generator. The construction depends only on a smooth non-extremal static solution manifold and the Fredholm properties of the reduced operator. A companion paper specializes the framework to the magnetic GHS family.

</details>


### [116] [Higher-Spin Correlators in dS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05278)
*Shounak De,Hayden Lee*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究dS₄中最小高阶自旋引力理论在动量空间的关联子，利用全息矢量模型描述。作者证明四点函数表现出非平凡的几何特征（Ptolemy关系），五点函数的表现奇异点（Landau奇异点）是虚假的并在Gram约束下消失，最终提出基于图论组合结构的高阶自旋关联子组合自举方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解dS₄中最小高阶自旋引力的全息关联子结构，特别是动量空间n点函数的几何与组合学特征，为建立一般多重数下的组合自举方案奠定基础。

Method: 利用全息矢量模型描述，将连通n点函数表示为三维单圈积分（有理函数）；对四点函数分析其Ptolemy几何关系；对五点函数考察五边形积分中的表观Landau奇异点，施加Gram约束后剔除虚假奇异点，并构建无虚假奇异点、以图论模块组织的表示；验证共形不变性并研究soft、collapsed和Ptolemy极限行为。

Result: 四点函数的物理奇异点由对偶动量四边形的Ptolemy关系控制；五点函数的表观前导Landau奇异点是虚假的，施加Gram约束后消失；获得了五点多点函数无虚假奇异点的图论构建块表示；验证了关联子的共形不变性并刻画了各类极限行为。

Conclusion: 该结构暗示了一种面向一般多重数高阶自旋关联子的组合自举方案，其中共形不变性和物理奇异点从底层图组合学中自然涌现。

Abstract: We study momentum-space correlators in minimal higher-spin gravity in $\mathrm{dS}_4$ using its holographic vector-model description. Connected $n$-point functions in this model are represented by three-dimensional one-loop integrals and are rational functions of the boundary kinematics. We show that the four-point function displays a nontrivial geometric feature: its physical singularity is governed by a Ptolemy relation for the dual momentum quadrilateral. At five points, the pentagon integral introduces an apparent leading Landau singularity, which we show is spurious and disappears once the Gram constraint is imposed. We derive a representation of the five-point function free of spurious singularities and organized in terms of graph-theoretic building blocks. We then verify the conformal invariance of these correlators and discuss their behavior in the soft, collapsed, and Ptolemy limits. This structure suggests a combinatorial bootstrap for higher-spin correlators at general multiplicity, in which conformal invariance and the physical singularities emerge from the underlying graph combinatorics.

</details>


### [117] [Mass and Force Relations for Extremal E2MD Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05280)
*Sera Cremonini,Mirjam Cvetič,Christopher N. Pope,Aritra Saha*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了爱因斯坦引力耦合膨胀子和两个麦克斯韦场中的静态极端黑洞，确定了任意维度下多黑洞构型的质量与相互作用能，并揭示了力抵消条件ab=-2(d-2)/(d-1)将吸引与排斥行为分开的普遍规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解极端黑洞之间的长程力行为是引力量子理论中的重要问题。此前的工作仅限于特定的膨胀耦合参数，作者希望在不显式构造黑洞几何的情况下，通过对称性和极值条件的普适方法分析任意参数(a,b)下非全同极端黑洞之间的相互作用力。

Method: 作者利用三个关键工具：(1) 在b=-1/a特殊轨迹上，利用时间对称初始值构造确定多黑洞构型的质量与相互作用能；(2) 利用膨胀子常位移对称性、齐次性和极值条件推导出一阶常微分方程，从而确定极端质量作为两个电荷的函数；(3) 通过在任意维度上推广以及使用精确的A2、B2和G2 Toda黑洞对符号模式进行验证。

Result: 在所有解析可控的区域中，b=-1/a（四维）或ab=-2(d-2)/(d-1)（任意维度）将吸引行为（b>-1/a）与排斥行为（b<-1/a）分开。在G2 Toda黑洞情形下，本应有问题的分支因包含视界外的裸奇点而被排除，揭示了正则性与长程力符号之间的有趣联系。

Conclusion: 极端黑洞之间的长程力符号存在一个与维度和膨胀耦合参数相关的普适判定条件，该条件无需显式构造黑洞几何即可通过对称性和极值条件导出。G2情形中裸奇点的排除暗示力的符号与几何正则性之间存在深层联系。

Abstract: We study static extremal black holes in Einstein gravity coupled to a dilaton and two Maxwell fields with independent dilaton couplings $a$ and $b$. When $b=-1/a$ in four dimensions, a time-symmetric initial-value construction allows us to determine the masses and interaction energies of multi-black-hole configurations. On this special locus, the long-range force between any two extremal black holes vanishes. For arbitrary $a$ and $b$, a constant dilaton-shift symmetry, together with homogeneity and the extremality condition, yields a first-order ordinary differential equation that determines the extremal mass as a function of the two electric charges. This equation allows us to analyze the force between non-identical extremal black holes without having to know the explicit black-hole geometry. In all analytically controlled regimes that we examine, the locus $b=-1/a$ separates attractive behavior for $b>-1/a$ from repulsive behavior for $b<-1/a$. We extend the analysis to arbitrary spacetime dimensions, where the corresponding force-cancellation condition is $ab=-2(d-2)/(d-1)$. Finally, we test this sign pattern using the exact $A_2$, $B_2$ and $G_2$ Toda black holes. In the $G_2$ case, a potentially problematic branch is excluded because it contains naked singularities outside the horizon, suggesting an intriguing connection between regularity and the sign of long-range forces.

</details>


### [118] [Scalar Hair at the String-Black-Hole Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06016)
*Eliseo Pavone*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究树级四维弦有效作用量中的静态球对称轴子-伸缩子解，证明其全族解可通过SL(2,R)轨道从纯伸缩子FJNW解获得，并刻画了轴子-伸缩子荷空间。作者分析了α'曲率修正与弦圈修正的微扰区，并使用弦-黑洞对应判据评估FJNW族在物理尺寸上的曲率诊断，发现所有非零标量毛分支在对应面上的曲率诊断均大于Schwarzschild。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解四维弦有效作用量中带标量毛（轴子和伸缩子）的静态球对称黑洞解的完整结构，并评估这些解在弦理论α'修正和高阶弦圈修正下的微扰有效性，特别关注弦-黑洞对应原则下这些解是否满足物理上可控的条件。

Method: 利用标量扇区的sigma模型结构，证明静态球对称渐近平坦解族是纯伸缩子FJNW解的SL(2,R)轨道，从而系统生成全部解并刻画荷空间。随后比较α'曲率修正与弦圈修正的起始条件，并在弦-黑洞对应面处评估局部α'曲率诊断量，将Schwarzschild分支设为归一化基准。

Result: Schwarzschild在对应面处恰好饱和名义α'阈值；每个带标量毛的FJNW分支的曲率诊断均高于该阈值。更一般地，与匹配方案中的共同整体归一化无关，所有非零毛分支在对应面的曲率诊断均大于Schwarzschild。远离对应点时，在足够弱的局部自引力和弱弦耦合条件下，标量毛外部区域可保持在α'控制之下。

Conclusion: 静态球对称轴子-伸缩子解的全族可由SL(2,R)对称性统一生成；在弦-黑洞对应判据下，Schwarzschild临界性最优，而标量毛解在对应面处始终偏离α'控制，但在远离对应点的长距离弱耦合区域仍可作为高激发弦态的微扰可控场。

Abstract: We study static, spherically symmetric axion--dilaton solutions of the tree-level four-dimensional string effective action. Using the sigma-model structure of the scalar sector, we show that the full family of static, spherically symmetric and asymptotically flat solutions is obtained as the $SL(2,\mathbb R)$ orbit of the pure-dilaton FJNW solution, and we characterize the associated axion--dilaton charge space. We then analyze the perturbative regime of these solutions by comparing the onset of $α'$ curvature corrections with that of string-loop corrections. Finally, we apply the string--black-hole correspondence criterion to the FJNW family by evaluating the local $α'$ curvature diagnostic at the physical size $R_{\rm typ}$ of a highly excited string state. With the normalization fixed on the Schwarzschild branch, Schwarzschild saturates the nominal $α'$ threshold at the correspondence surface, whereas every scalar-haired FJNW branch lies above it. More generally, independently of the common overall normalization within the matching prescription, every nonzero-hair branch has a larger curvature diagnostic at the correspondence surface than Schwarzschild. Away from the correspondence point, scalar-haired exteriors may remain under $α'$ control at sufficiently weak local self-gravity and may provide perturbatively controlled long-distance fields of highly excited string states whenever the local string coupling also remains weak.

</details>


### [119] [$N$-Photon Amplitudes in EFT from Recursion Relations and Effective Vertices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05285)
*Gustavo Bispo,Sylvain Fichet*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个递归框架，用于计算一般电磁有效场论（EFT）中任意树级螺旋度振幅。利用类似CSW的递归方法，光子振幅的构建块是纯局域的子振幅（接触振幅），这些接触振幅可用Hafnian紧凑表示，并由有效接触拉格朗日量系统编码。论文提供了直到N=14光子的接触拉格朗日量，证明了接触拉格朗日量揭示了螺旋度守恒振幅的隐藏简单性，给出了离壳电磁对偶性与螺旋度守恒之间等价的简单证明，并展示了如何对所有N求和螺旋度守恒理论的接触拉格朗日量（以Born-Infeld电磁学为例）。最后，论文计算了通用EFT算子产生的完整6点和8点树级螺旋度振幅，以及BI电磁学中至10点的树级振幅。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高阶电磁EFT中计算树级散射振幅通常非常复杂且耗时，特别是随着外部腿数增加，Feynman图数量爆炸式增长。现有的递归方法（如BCFW、CSW）在规范理论中取得了巨大成功，但在一般EFT中的推广尚不清楚。作者希望建立一种系统的、可扩展的框架，能够高效计算任意阶EFT算子的光子振幅，同时揭示螺旋度振幅中隐藏的数学结构（如Hafnian表示和接触拉格朗日量），并澄清电磁对偶性与螺旋度守恒之间的深层联系。

Method: (1) 建立CSW-like递归框架：光子振幅被递归地构建，构建块是纯局域的接触子振幅；(2) 接触振幅的Hafnian表示：对于固定外部腿数，只需计算有限数量的接触振幅，这些振幅可用Hafnian函数紧凑表达；(3) 有效接触拉格朗日量：通过适当的生成泛函从一般EFT拉格朗日量系统地计算接触拉格朗日量，并给出至N=14光子的显式结果；(4) 螺旋度守恒理论的处理：证明接触拉格朗日量给出了离壳电磁对偶性与螺旋度守恒等价的简单证明，并展示了如何对所有N求和（以Born-Infeld为例说明）；(5) 具体计算：计算了通用EFT算子的完整6点和8点树级振幅，以及BI电磁学至10点的树级振幅。

Result: 作者成功构建了电磁EFT中树级螺旋度振幅的递归计算框架，得到了直到N=14光子的接触拉格朗日量紧凑表达式。通过接触拉格朗日量，给出了离壳电磁对偶性与螺旋度守恒等价的简洁证明，并实现了螺旋度守恒理论接触拉格朗日量的全N求和（以Born-Infeld为例）。此外，完成了通用EFT算子的6点和8点完整树级振幅计算，以及BI电磁学中至10点的树级振幅。

Conclusion: 本文建立的递归框架为一般电磁EFT中的树级振幅计算提供了一种高效且系统的工具，揭示了接触拉格朗日量在理解螺旋度振幅结构中的核心作用。该框架不仅简化了高阶EFT算子的振幅计算，还建立了电磁对偶性与螺旋度守恒之间的明确等价关系。这项工作为更广泛的EFT振幅方法研究奠定了基础，其Hafnian结构和接触拉格朗日量技术有望推广到其他规范理论和有效场论中。

Abstract: We present a recursive framework for computing arbitrary tree-level helicity amplitudes in the general effective field theory (EFT) of electromagnetism. We show that photon amplitudes can be constructed using a CSW-like recursion, whose building blocks consist of purely local subamplitudes. For a fixed number of external legs, only a finite number of these contact amplitudes need to be computed, and these admit compact expressions in terms of Hafnians. We further show that the contact amplitudes are encoded in an effective contact Lagrangian, that can be computed systematically from the general EFT Lagrangian using a suitable generating functional. We provide this contact Lagrangian up to $N=14$ photons. We argue that the contact Lagrangian reveals the hidden simplicity of helicity-conserving amplitudes, providing a simple proof of the equivalence between off-shell electromagnetic duality and helicity conservation. We further show how to resum the contact Lagrangian of a helicity-conserving theory to all $N$, and illustrate the procedure for Born-Infeld (BI) electromagnetism. Building on these methods and results, we compute the complete 6-point and 8-point tree-level helicity amplitudes generated by generic EFT operators, and the tree amplitudes up to 10-point in BI electromagnetism.

</details>


### [120] [Partition Functions of Hermitian and PT-Symmetric Oscillators from Integrable Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06047)
*Hongfei Shu,Jingjing Yang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文基于ODE/IM对应关系，为齐次Hermitian和PT对称谐振子建立了热配分函数与谱Zeta函数的积分表示，通过Destri-de Vega方程求解计数函数a(E)，实现量子谱函数与可积模型之间的直接联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法计算谐振子热配分函数和谱Zeta函数较为复杂，论文希望通过ODE/IM对应这一可积模型工具，建立量子谱函数与可积模型之间的桥梁，从而提供一种统一的、新的计算框架。

Method: 利用ODE/IM对应关系，将两类谐振子的量子化条件通过计数函数a(E)表达，并通过Destri-de Vega方程求解该函数。随后将配分函数和谱Zeta函数表示为包含计数函数的围道积分，从而将量子谱函数与可积模型联系起来。

Result: 成功建立了Hermitian与PT对称齐次谐振子的配分函数和谱Zeta函数的围道积分表示，这些表示均通过计数函数a(E)实现，而a(E)可由Destri-de Vega方程求解。

Conclusion: 该工作为量子谱函数的计算提供了基于可积模型的新途径，展示了ODE/IM对应在量子统计物理和谱理论中的广泛应用潜力。

Abstract: We develop an ODE/IM-based formulation for the thermal partition function and the spectral zeta function of the homogeneous Hermitian and PT-symmetric oscillators. For both classes of systems, the quantization condition can be expressed using the counting function $a(E)$, which can be solved via the Destri-de Vega equation of the integrable model. We then express the partition function and spectral zeta function as contour integrals involving the counting function, thereby providing a direct bridge between quantum spectral functions and integrable models.

</details>


### [121] [Adiabatic Deformations of Black Hole Moduli: II. The Magnetic GHS Family](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05291)
*Karim Benakli,Anna Chrysostomou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了无质量Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton理论中磁性GHS黑洞对慢变外部模量的绝热响应。利用内向Eddington-Finkelstein坐标和精确面积半径，作者围绕缓慢演化的瞬时静态GHS代表解展开时间依赖球对称方程，通过规范切片与Fredholm框架解析地求得了准静态近区响应的完整闭式解，并在大半径重叠区间内证明了Fredholm分母严格为正，从而确立了存在唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解黑洞在外部参数缓慢变化时的绝热响应是广义相对论和非平衡引力物理中的核心问题。作者旨在为磁性GHS黑洞建立一种严格而可控的扰动态框架，弥补此前在带荷黑洞绝热响应研究中缺乏解析精确解和存在唯一性证明的空白。

Method: 采用内向Eddington-Finkelstein坐标并保持精确面积半径，推导时间依赖的球对称场方程；将动态解围绕缓慢演化的瞬时静态GHS代表展开，区别处理精确动力学视界与瞬时静态视界。利用GHS族的可积结构，在规范切片和Fredholm框架下闭式构造：规范切线、有限秩源剖面、辅助非齐次方程的水平规整解、以及切片算子核的整体规整生成元；解析求值大半径重叠区Fredholm分母并证明其在非极值带电分支上严格为正。最后显式求解受迫径向方程，以外部匹配数据固定切片齐次模振幅，由径向约束和单次初等积分分别得到质量滞后和lapse滞后。

Result: 作者解析求得了完整的leading阶准静态近区响应：(1) Fredholm分母在带电非极值分支严格为正，证明了存在唯一性；(2) 在核生成元常规归一化退化处，该处实为归一化奇点而非核模简并；(3) 标量滞后分解为普适受迫剖面加切片齐次模（振幅由外部匹配数据决定）；(4) 质量滞后由径向约束代数给出，lapse滞后由单一初等积分给出；(5) 精确边际视界可从滞后修正解恢复。所有径向依赖均为精确的。

Conclusion: 该工作为磁性GHS黑洞在无质量大半径重叠模型内提供了完整的leading准静态绝热响应：严格的Fredholm正性确立了问题良定性，闭式解给出了任意允许外部数据下的完整径向精确响应。结果还澄清了核生成元归一化奇点与真正简并的本质区别，为后续更广泛的带荷黑洞准静态响应研究奠定了方法论基础。

Abstract: We study the leading adiabatic response of magnetic GHS black holes in massless Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton theory to a prescribed exterior modulus varying slowly in advanced time. In ingoing Eddington-Finkelstein coordinates with the exact areal radius, we derive the time-dependent spherical equations and expand about a slowly evolving instantaneous static GHS representative, keeping its horizon distinct from the exact dynamical marginal horizon. The magnetic GHS family realizes the canonical-slice and Fredholm framework developed in the companion paper. Its integrability yields in closed form the canonical tangent, the finite-rank source profile, a horizon-regular solution of the auxiliary inhomogeneous equation, and a globally regular generator of the sliced-operator kernel. For the leading Robin functional arising in the massless large-radius overlap, we evaluate the Fredholm denominator analytically and prove that it is strictly positive on the charged non-extremal branch. This remains true where the conventional normalization of the kernel generator degenerates, showing that these loci are normalization singularities rather than degeneracies of the kernel mode. Within the controlled large-radius overlap regime, this positivity proves existence and uniqueness of the completed leading-order quasi-static near-zone problem. We then solve the forced radial equation explicitly. The scalar lag is the sum of a universal forced profile and a sliced homogeneous mode whose amplitude is fixed by the exterior matching datum. The mass lag follows algebraically from the radial constraint, and the lapse lag from a single elementary quadrature. The exact marginal horizon is recovered from the lag-corrected solution. The result gives the complete leading quasi-static near-zone response, exact in its radial dependence, for every admissible exterior datum within the specified massless overlap model.

</details>


### [122] [Interacting Galilean and Finite-Energy Carroll Fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05324)
*Pulastya Parekh,Aditya Sharma,Mauricio Valenzuela*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过一个依赖于质量和光速的相似变换，统一推导了有质量狄拉克作用的伽利略极限和卡罗尔极限。通过不同的狄拉克共轭选择与质量标度组合，产生了不同的极限作用族，恢复了已知的伽利略和卡罗尔费米子模型，并得到了各自领域的新系统。文章还分类了与boost相容的局域（自）相互作用（包括NJL），并揭示了新伽利略自由作用具有局域费米子规范对称性，而选定的四体相互作用会显式破缺该对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有文献中伽利略和卡罗尔极限的费米子模型虽然已有一些分散的构造，但缺乏一个统一的推导框架。作者旨在通过一个基于相似变换的统一方法，系统地生成两类极限下的狄拉克作用，并揭示其规范对称性结构与相互作用分类。

Method: 采用一个依赖质量和光速的相似变换对有质量狄拉克作用进行变换，结合两种狄拉克共轭选择（伽利略型和卡罗尔型）与不同的质量标度方案，取光速极限得到不同族的作用。随后研究这些作用的boost相容的局域（自）相互作用分类，并分析各作用中的规范对称性和能量参数的可消除性。

Result: 恢复已知的伽利略和卡罗尔费米子模型并各获得一个新模型。新伽利略自由作用具有局域费米子规范对称性，从而消除了物理场内容；四体相互作用可显式破缺该对称性使场内容得以保留，而NJL极限作用仅形变该规范对称性的实现。多个卡罗尔作用中的能量参数可通过含时相位重定义消除，但新卡罗尔扩展中的能量参数不能被消除。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个统一且系统化的框架来构造和研究非相对论极限（伽利略）和超相对论极限（卡罗尔）下的费米子系统，揭示了不同极限下规范对称性和场内容结构的丰富差异，并展示了相互作用如何影响这些对称性性质。

Abstract: We present a unified derivation of the Galilean and Carrollian limits of the massive Dirac action based on a similarity transformation depending on the mass and the speed of light. The two choices of Dirac conjugation, combined with different mass scalings, generate distinct families of limiting actions. This construction recovers known Galilei and Carroll fermion models and yields new systems, one in each regime. We classify a sufficient set of boost-compatible local (self-)interactions, which includes Nambu--Jona-Lasinio (NJL). The new Galilean free action possesses a local fermionic gauge symmetry that eliminates its local field content. A selected quartic interaction, preserving the Bargmann boost, explicitly breaks the original fermionic gauge symmetry so that the argument that eliminates the field content no longer applies. The NJL limit interaction merely deforms the realization of that gauge symmetry. We show that the energy parameter in several Carroll actions can be eliminated by a time-dependent phase redefinition; conversely, in the new Carrollian extension the energy cannot be removed.

</details>


### [123] [Surviving correlations across a horizon: reflected entropy for bosonic fields in non-inertial frames and black hole spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05473)
*Sayid Mondal*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了自由玻色子场在惯性观测者（Alice、Charlie）与匀加速观测者（Bob）之间共享模的反射熵与马尔可夫间隙，涵盖了二分Bell态及三分的Werner和GHZ态。通过识别守恒荷将约化密度矩阵块对角化为精确的二维扇区，作者发现尽管玻色子纠缠在渐进上消失，Alice-Bob反射熵却饱和于非零值，保留了存活的经典关联。关键区别在于楔间反射熵随挤压参数线性发散（而费米子情形保持有界），马尔可夫间隙则饱和。该构造可直接移植到Schwarzschild黑洞中，饱和值成为与质量无关的常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在理解非惯性观测者（匀加速/黑洞）视角下自由玻色子场中反射熵与马尔可夫间隙的行为，特别是与费米子情形的对比。由于玻色子的Bogoliubov变换跨越无限维Fock空间且挤压参数无界，分析更具挑战性，需要通过对称性简化以获取精确或半解析结果。

Method: 作者利用Bogoliubov变换描述加速观测者与惯性观测者之间的模耦合，通过识别系统的守恒荷，将无限维的约化密度矩阵块对角化到精确的二维扇区，从而对所有三种态（Bell、W、GHZ）得出闭合或半解析表达式。这一构造还被推广到Schwarzschild黑洞背景。

Result: 玻色子纠缠在渐进上消失，但Alice-Bob反射熵饱和于非零的饱和平台，收敛到Alice与Bob因果断开伙伴共享的值。只有楔间反射熵线性发散于挤压参数，这与费米子情形（有界）形成鲜明对比；马尔可夫间隙饱和。在Schwarzschild黑洞中饱和值为与质量无关的常数。

Conclusion: 玻色子场中反射熵揭示了一种不同于纠缠的熵结构：即使量子纠缠消失，反射熵仍能捕获存活的经典关联。楔间反射熵的发散行为是玻色子情形独有的普适特征，且该结果在黑洞背景下以质量无关的饱和常数形式出现，暗示了某种普适性。

Abstract: We study the reflected entropy and the Markov gap for modes of a free bosonic field shared by inertial observers (Alice, Charlie) and a uniformly accelerated one (Bob), for a bipartite Bell state and the tripartite Werner (W) and Greenberger--Horne--Zeilinger (GHZ) states. The bosonic Bogoliubov transformation spans an infinite-dimensional Fock space with an unbounded squeezing parameter unlike the fermionic case. By identifying a conserved charge, we block-diagonalize the reduced density matrices into exact two-dimensional sectors, yielding closed or semi-analytic forms for all three states. Although bosonic entanglement is known to vanish asymptotically, the Alice--Bob reflected entropy instead saturates at a nonzero saturation floor, retaining the surviving classical correlation, and converges to the value Alice shares with Bob's causally disconnected partner. Crucially, only the inter-wedge reflected entropy diverges, linearly in the squeezing parameter---the sharp distinction from the fermionic case, where it stays bounded---while the Markov gaps saturate. The construction transfers verbatim to a Schwarzschild black hole, where the saturation values become mass-independent constants.

</details>


### [124] [From chiral to conformal anomalies: a double copy perspective for CFT correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05731)
*Claudio Corianò,Stefano Lionetti*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一个双重拷贝（double-copy）关系，将四维共形场论中的手征反常与共形反常联系起来。该关系不仅存在于局部反常结构中，还扩展到完整的动量空间三点关联函数及其平空间极限下的五维散射振幅。手征反常的奇宇称流关联函数在平空间极限下约化为由Chern-Simons相互作用产生的普适振幅，对这些关联函数取平方可重现由共形反常控制的能动量张量三点函数<TTT>的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索手征反常与共形反常之间深层的数学结构联系，并寻找它们通过双重拷贝关系在散射振幅层面的统一描述，从而深化对量子场论中反常结构的理解。

Method: 通过分析动量空间中手征反常对应的奇宇称流三点关联函数与共形反常控制的能动量张量三点关联函数<TTT>，利用双重拷贝构造（平方关系）建立两者的对应。该方法从局部反常结构出发，延伸至完整的三点关联函数，并借助平空间极限将结果与五维散射振幅（尤其是Chern-Simons振幅）建立联系。

Result: 建立了手征反常与共形反常之间的双重拷贝关系，该关系从局部反常结构扩展到完整的动量空间三点关联函数及平空间极限下的五维散射振幅。具体而言，手征反常的奇宇称流关联函数在平空间极限下约化为由Chern-Simons相互作用产生的普适振幅，且其平方（双重拷贝）可精确重现共形反常控制的<TTT>贡献。

Conclusion: 手征反常与共形反常之间存在非平凡的普适双重拷贝关系，这为反常结构在量子场论与散射振幅之间架起了新的桥梁，暗示反常系统可能具有更深层的振幅层面结构。

Abstract: We uncover a double-copy relation between chiral and conformal anomalies in four-dimensional conformal field theory. We show that this relation extends from local anomaly structures to complete momentum-space three-point correlators and, through their flat-space limits, to five-dimensional scattering amplitudes. In particular, the parity-odd current correlators associated with the chiral anomaly reduce, in the flat-space limit, to a universal amplitude generated by a Chern--Simons interaction. Squaring these anomalous current correlators reproduces the contribution to the stress-tensor three-point function $\langle TTT\rangle$, controlled by the conformal anomaly, thereby establishing a direct link between chiral- and conformal-anomaly structures.

</details>


### [125] [Information-Theoretic Black Hole Entropy I: Beyond the Area Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05795)
*Alex Kehagias*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文指出Bekenstein-Hawking面积定律与热力学第三定律（Nernst形式）相冲突，并认为这种张力源于半经典面积定律的局限而非黑洞热力学的基本特征。作者提出一个新的熵公式，在低温极限下满足第三定律，在高温极限下恢复Bekenstein-Hawking熵。该熵可解释为质量偏置伯努利分布与N个微观比特上均匀分布之间的Kullback-Leibler散度，即黑洞系综与最大混合参考系综之间的相对熵（熵亏损）。Bekenstein-Hawking面积定律作为1/N展开的首项出现，普适质量标度M0=√N·MP作为黑洞质量的绝对上界控制微观系综的偏置，次领头项代表对经典面积定律的有限信息修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞热力学的前两条定律（第一、第二定律）与Bekenstein-Hawking面积定律一致，但该面积定律与热力学第三定律（Nernst表述：零温度极限下熵应趋于零或普适常数）存在明显冲突。作者认为这一冲突源于半经典面积定律本身的局限性，而非黑洞热力学的根本缺陷，因此需要构造一个与第三定律相容的新熵公式。

Method: 作者推导出一个同时满足热力学第三定律（低温极限）和恢复Bekenstein-Hawking面积定律（高温极限）的新熵公式。该公式具有清晰的微观诠释：将熵表示为质量偏置Bernoulli分布与N个微观比特上均匀分布之间的Kullback-Leibler散度，从而将热力学黑洞熵视为熵亏损（相对熵），即黑洞系综相对于最大混合参考系综的信息论熵。通过1/N展开，面积定律作为首项出现，由普适质量标度M0=√N·MP控制的偏置参数决定次领头修正项。

Result: 获得的熵公式在低温极限下与第三定律相容，在高温极限下逼近Bekenstein-Hawking熵。该熵具有信息论诠释（KL散度/相对熵），Bekenstein-Hawking面积定律作为1/N展开的首项自然涌现，次领头项给出对经典面积定律的有限信息修正。同时引入了普适质量标度M0=√N·MP作为黑洞质量的绝对上界。

Conclusion: Bekenstein-Hawking面积定律与第三定律的冲突源于半经典近似的局限而非黑洞热力学的根本问题。新提出的熵公式统一了热力学（第三定律）和信息论（熵亏损/相对熵）两个视角，为黑洞熵提供了更完整的微观描述，其中面积定律仅作为大N极限下的首项近似。

Abstract: Although the Bekenstein-Hawking area law is consistent with the first and second laws of black hole thermodynamics, it appears to be in conflict with the third law, which in the Nernst formulation states that the entropy should either vanish or approach a universal constant in the zero-temperature limit. We argue that this tension reflects a limitation of the semiclassical area law rather than a fundamental feature of black hole thermodynamics. On this basis, we obtain an entropy formula that is consistent with the third law, and approaches Bekenstein-Hawking entropy in the high-temperature limit. The resulting entropy admits a simple microscopic interpretation, and it can be written as the Kullback-Leibler divergence between a mass-biased Bernoulli distribution and the uniform distribution on N microscopic bits. From this perspective, the thermodynamic black hole entropy admits an information-theoretic representation as an entropy deficit, namely as the relative entropy between the black hole ensemble and a maximally mixed reference ensemble. The Bekenstein-Hawking area law then emerges as the leading term in an $1/N$ expansion, while a universal mass scale $M_0=\sqrt{N}\,M_P$, interpreted as an absolute upper bound on the black hole mass, controls the bias of the underlying microscopic ensemble. The subleading terms represent finite-information corrections to the classical area law.

</details>


### [126] [Information--Theoretic Black Hole Entropy II: Infrared Gravity and Charged/Rotating Extensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05801)
*Alex Kehagias*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文研究一种满足热力学第三定律（Nernst表述）的信息论黑洞熵，将其作为Kullback-Leibler散度而非微观态数目对数，并证明其可通过广义相对论的红外形变在微扰层面复现，同时基于不可约质量扩展到Kerr-Newman黑洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统黑洞熵基于微观态计数（Bekenstein-Hawking熵），且在极端黑洞中可能违反热力学第三定律的Nernst表述。作者希望通过信息论熵重新构建黑洞熵，使其满足第三定律，并探索其在引力理论中的实现。

Method: 将黑洞熵定义为质量相关伯努利系综与无偏参考系综之间的Kullback-Leibler散度；通过广义相对论的红外形变的微扰展开，在线性阶匹配表面引力温度与Wald熵；并利用不可约质量将构造推广至Kerr-Newman黑洞。

Result: 线性阶红外形变可再现信息论温度与熵；显式实现中产生正的有效宇宙学常数，其小值与微观参数N相关；基于不可约质量的Kerr-Newman扩展保留了与视界面积和半经典极限的联系，同时区分了几何极端性与与角动量、电荷无关的普适统计冻结端点。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了满足第三定律的信息论黑洞熵与广义相对论红外形变之间的联系，并提出一个能统一描述Schwarzschild与Kerr-Newman黑洞的熵框架，其中统计冻结（第三定律）是普适的，而几何极端性则依赖于角动量和电荷。

Abstract: We investigate gravitational and Kerr-Newman extensions of an information-theoretic black-hole entropy that satisfies the Nernst formulation of the third law of thermodynamics. The entropy is identified with the Kullback-Leibler divergence between a mass-dependent Bernoulli ensemble and an unbiased reference ensemble, and therefore measures relative information rather than the logarithm of the number of black-hole microstates. We show perturbatively that its temperature and entropy can be reproduced by an infrared deformation of General Relativity. To linear order in the deformation couplings, the surface-gravity temperature and the Wald entropy agree with the information-theoretic results. In an explicit realization, the matching generates a positive effective cosmological term whose smallness is related to the large microscopic parameter $N$. We also propose an extension to Kerr-Newman black holes based on the irreducible mass. This construction preserves the connection with the horizon area and the semiclassical limit, while distinguishing geometrical extremality from the universal statistical-freezing endpoint, which is independent of angular momentum and charge.

</details>


### [127] [Emergent gravitational action from non-local $T\bar T$-like deformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05913)
*Yun-Ze Li,Bo-Rui Li,Yu-Xiao Liu,Song He*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了非局域$T\bar T$型形变量子场论在形变参数一阶诱导的引力有效作用量，通过热核方法提取应力张量两点函数产生的局部几何项，并将其应用于自由费米子、大质量Maxwell理论及二阶Yang-Mills理论。研究发现在共形场论中存在由中心荷$C_T$固定的普适扇区，经正则化和重整化后产生有限且与方案无关的曲率不变量贡献；同时分析了迹-迹形变中由Weyl反常决定的接触项，推导了最小非局域核的一般引力有效作用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非局域$T\bar T$形变为量子场论提供了新的物理内涵，但其诱导的引力有效作用量（即几何响应）尚未得到系统理解。本文旨在建立一套系统框架，从量子场论应力张量关联函数出发提取普适的引力有效作用量，特别关注共形场论中由中心荷决定的通用结构。

Method: 采用热核方法（heat-kernel formulation），在非局域$T\bar T$型形变参数的一阶展开下，从应力张量两点函数提取局部几何项。通过正则化和重整化处理发散，对自由费米子、大质量Maxwell理论和二阶Yang-Mills理论分别构建其引力有效作用量。对迹-迹形变情形，利用Weyl反常确定相关接触项，推导最小非局域核类的一般有限作用量。

Result: 发现各模型中诱导的引力系数一般依赖于具体模型，但共形场论中存在由中心荷$C_T$唯一确定的普适扇区；该扇区经正则化和重整化后给出有限的、方案无关的贡献，并组织为有限组曲率不变量。迹-迹形变情形中，接触项由Weyl反常决定，并得到了对应于一般最小非局域核的有限引力有效作用量。

Conclusion: 该工作为诱导引力的量子有效作用量提供了具体实现：普适的共形数据（如中心荷$C_T$）能够确定可计算的、对涌现几何响应的贡献。这一框架为理解非局域形变与引力之间的深层联系奠定了方法论基础。

Abstract: We study the gravitational effective action induced, at first order in the deformation parameter, by non-local $T\bar T$-like deformations of quantum field theories. Using a heat-kernel formulation, we extract the local geometric terms generated by stress-tensor two-point functions, and apply the construction to free fermions, massive Maxwell theory, and second-order Yang-Mills theory. While the resulting coefficients are generally model dependent, conformal field theories contain a universal sector fixed by the central charge $C_T$. After regularization and renormalization, this sector yields finite, scheme-independent contributions organized into a finite set of curvature invariants. We further analyze trace-trace deformations, for which the relevant contact terms are determined by the Weyl anomaly, and derive the corresponding finite gravitational action for a general class of minimal non-local kernels. These results provide a quantum effective-action realization of induced gravity in which universal conformal data determine calculable contributions to the emergent geometric response.

</details>


### [128] [Three-Loop Five-Point CK-Dual Amplitudes and UV Structure in N=4 SYM and N=8 SUGRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05973)
*Zhiming Cai,Zeyu Li,Gang Yang,Guorui Zhu*

TL;DR: 本文构建了N=4超杨-米尔斯理论的三圈五点完整全色积分项，其表示形式满足颜色-运动学对偶，并通过双重拷贝得到相应的N=8超引力积分项。在临界维度Dc=6下评估了两者的紫外极点，并探讨了将外部态依赖性扩展到D维度的微妙问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究N=4超杨-米尔斯和N=8超引力的非平面高圈积分项，特别是验证颜色-运动学对偶在更高圈阶的成立性，并理解这两类理论在紫外行为上的关联与差异，这对研究超引力的紫外有限性具有重要意义。

Method: 采用能显式保持颜色-运动学对偶的表示来构造三圈五点完整积分项，通过双重拷贝变换生成N=8超引力积分项。在Dc=6临界维度下计算紫外极点，并用D维树级振幅构造替代四维prefactor的候选表达式，与弦理论预言进行对比。

Result: 该候选D维表达式成功复现了SYM在一般D维外部态下的开弦预言极点，但相应的引力表达式与弦启发式结果存在一个消失项（evanescent term）的差异。

Conclusion: 颜色-运动学对偶可用于构造更高圈的完整积分项，但对D维外部态的延拓存在微妙问题，N=8超引力与弦理论预言在一般维度下存在evanescent差异，暗示需要对四维prefactor的D维扩展做更细致的处理。

Abstract: We construct the complete full-color three-loop five-point integrand of N=4 super-Yang--Mills theory in a representation that manifestly satisfies color--kinematics duality. Its double copy gives the corresponding N=8 supergravity integrand. For four-dimensional external states, we evaluate the ultraviolet poles of both amplitudes in the critical dimension, Dc=6. Extending the external-state dependence to D dimensions is subtle. We consider a candidate replacement of the four-dimensional prefactors by expressions built from D-dimensional tree amplitudes. It reproduces the open-string prediction for the SYM pole with generic D-dimensional external states, whereas the corresponding gravity expression differs from the string-inspired one by an evanescent term.

</details>


### [129] [Integrable models of inflation beyond slow-roll](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06071)
*M. Bianchi,G. Dibitetto,J. F. Morales,V. Zevola*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的解析方法，用于研究多组分FLRW宇宙学及其扰动。通过将标量势用Hubble函数H(φ)（作为假超势）表达，将动力学转化为一阶问题，从而在合适的时间坐标下解析求解。该方法统一了标准大爆炸宇宙学、暴胀模型和暗能量模型，并通过解析与数值方法求解Mukhanov-Sasaki方程，计算功率谱、谱指数和张量-标量比，与观测及慢滚近似预测进行比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的慢滚近似虽然被广泛使用，但对宇宙学扰动的计算存在局限。作者旨在建立一个统一的解析框架，能够精确处理多组分FLRW宇宙学中的暴胀及扰动演化，超越慢滚近似的限制，并与现代观测数据（如CMB功率谱）进行更精确的比较。

Method: 1) 将单一标量场的势函数编码多组分流体的能量密度和压力，统一描述标准大爆炸、暴胀和暗能量模型。2) 将标量势表示为Hubble函数H(φ)的形式，H(φ)充当假超势（fake superpotential），使动力学降为一阶问题。3) 在合适的演化时间坐标下解析求解背景动力学。4) 利用数值和半解析技术积分Mukhanov-Sasaki方程，计算标量和张量宇宙学扰动的功率谱、谱指数及张量-标量比。

Result: 提出了具有文献中类似性质且与观测相容的可积暴胀模型；通过求解Mukhanov-Sasaki方程计算了标量和张量扰动的功率谱、谱指数与张量-标量比，其结果超越了慢滚近似的适用范围，并与当前观测数据进行了对比。

Conclusion: 该解析框架为多组分FLRW宇宙学及其扰动提供了一种可积且统一的研究工具，能够在超越慢滚近似的水平上精确计算宇宙学观测量，为暴胀模型与观测的对比提供了更严格的检验平台。

Abstract: We propose a novel analytic approach to the study of multi-component FLRW cosmologies and their perturbations. The dynamics is triggered by a single scalar field with a scalar potential codifying the energy density and pressure of the multi-component fluid. This description unifies standard Big Bang cosmologies, models of inflation and dark energy under a unique framework. The key to integrability is to express the scalar potential in terms of the Hubble function $H(φ)$ that plays the role of a fake superpotential, turning the dynamics into a first order problem, that may be analytically solved in a suitable time coordinate. In this framework, we propose integrable inflationary models with similar properties to the ones analysed in the literature and compatible with observations. Finally, we study scalar and tensor cosmological perturbations in each model by integrating Mukhanov-Sasaki equations via numerical and (semi-)analytic techniques. This allow us to compute the power spectrum, the spectral indices and the tensor-to-scalar ratio beyond the slow-roll approximation and compare our general results {against} the currently available observations and the theoretical predictions based on the slow-roll approximation.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究超对称t-禁闭规范理论在小反常媒介超对称破缺下的行为，目的是通过识别全局对称性破缺模式，为与非超对称理论的格点模拟结果进行比较提供理论依据。

Method: 采用反常媒介（anomaly mediation）机制引入小的超对称破缺，对超对称t-禁闭（truly confining）规范理论进行形变分析，并系统地识别形变后各理论中全局对称性的破缺模式。

Result: 成功识别了在加入小反常媒介超对称破缺后，各超对称t-禁闭规范理论中全局对称性的破缺类型和模式；其中一种情况（赝实且手征的理论）除外，其他结果均可与非超对称理论的格点模拟进行原则上的比较。

Conclusion: 通过小反常媒介超对称破缺对t-禁闭理论的形变研究，获得了全局对称性破缺模式，这为连接超对称与非超对称禁闭理论提供了可行途径，并可与格点模拟进行对比验证。

Abstract: We study deformation of supersymmetric $t$-confining (truly confininig) gauge theories with small anomaly mediation of supersymmetry breaking. We identify breaking pattern of global symmetries in the theories. These results can be compared in priciple to lattice simulation of non-supersymmetric theories (except for one of the cases where the theory is pseudoreal and chiral).

</details>
