<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望利用AMSB与非超对称极限属于同一普适类的猜想，来理解非超对称赝实约束规范理论的低能物理，尤其是检验tumbling机制对该类理论谱的预期是否正确。

Method: 采用AMSB形变超对称规范理论的方法，借助超对称工具（如超对称量子动力学、约束谱分析）推断非超对称极限下的粒子谱，并系统检查非标准tumbling是否可能调和两种图景。

Result: 发现非超对称赝实约束规范理论中没有无质量粒子，与近期tumbling预期相矛盾；非标准tumbling在三个情形中需要排斥通道凝聚，且有一个情形无论吸引力/排斥性如何均无法实现调和；对可能处于共形窗口的附加理论，其超对称版本与流向超共形不动点一致。

Conclusion: 赝实约束非超对称规范理论的谱中不存在无质量粒子，tumbling图景与该结论难以调和，且部分情形不可能调和；同时确认了候选共形窗口理论在超对称版本中的一致性。

Abstract: I analyze the pseudoreal confining $\mathcal{N}=1$ SUSY gauge theories deformed by anomaly mediated SUSY breaking (AMSB). Taking the conjecture that the AMSB and non-SUSY limits are in the same universality class, I find broadly that the non-SUSY confining pseudoreal gauge theories of this class have no massless particles in their spectra. This contradicts recent expectations from tumbling. I explore whether or not non-standard tumbling could reconcile the two pictures, and find that it would require condensation in repulsive channels in three of the cases, and that in one case it does not appear possible regardless of attractiveness/repulsiveness. I briefly comment on the additional theories that may be in the conformal window, and find that their SUSY versions are consistent with flowing to superconformal fixed points.

</details>


### [132] [Isomorphic Emergence of Lorentz and Gauge Symmetries--A Constructive Interpretation Based on Continuum Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06244)
*Ke-Xia Jiang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从构造性诠释出发，证明洛伦兹对称性和规范对称性可以从一个统一的经典来源——连续弹性基底介质（SM）中波包激发的动力学约束——同构地涌现出来，无需将它们视为基本对称性，也不依赖量子化过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 洛伦兹对称性和规范对称性是现代物理学的数学基石，但其最终物理起源仍是谜团。作者试图从构造性诠释的角度为这两种对称结构提供一个统一的经典起源解释。

Method: 论文通过三个核心论证：（1）以均匀各向同性弹性介质的横波速度为基准，在约定主义同步方案下同构地导出闵可夫斯基时空和洛伦兹变换；（2）对具有SU(N)内禀对称性的波包，通过同一性不变性要求驱动介质自发产生规范场，得到与杨-米尔斯理论同构的规范结构；（3）在相同约定主义测量协议下，介质的非均匀分布同构地涌现为弯曲时空几何，并通过介质形变自由能的变分极值导出爱因斯坦型场方程。

Result: 建立了三个核心结果：闵可夫斯基型时空及洛伦兹变换的同构涌现（重新诠释了迈克尔逊-莫雷零结果）；与杨-米尔斯理论同构的规范场自发生成（规范群SU(N)由介质支持的稳定简正模式数唯一确定）；弯曲时空几何及爱因斯坦型场方程的同构涌现。整体上提供了一个统一诠释框架。

Conclusion: 该论文提出了一种统一的诠释性说明，将相对论和标准模型的数学结构的物理起源归结为弹性基底介质中波包激发的动力学约束，而非基本对称性假设，为理解现代物理两大理论体系的深层联系提供了一个新的构造性视角。

Abstract: Lorentz symmetry and gauge symmetry constitute the mathematical cornerstones of modern physics, yet their ultimate physical origins remain elusive. From the standpoint of a constructive interpretation, this paper demonstrates that both symmetry structures can emerge isomorphically from a unified classical source: dynamical constraints on wave-packet excitations in a continuous elastic substrate medium (SM). Neither symmetry is posited as fundamental, nor does their emergence rely on quantization. Three core results are established. First, taking the transverse wave speed of a homogeneous, isotropic SM as the benchmark under a conventionalist synchronization scheme, Minkowski-type spacetime arises isomorphically, with Lorentz transformations as effective coordinate transformations between inertial frames. This reinterprets the Michelson--Morley null result: observers are composite wave-packet excitations, and medium-induced kinematic corrections are encoded in their measurement frameworks. Second, for wave packets endowed with $SU(N)$ intrinsic symmetry, the requirement of identity invariance drives the SM to spontaneously generate gauge fields. This requires invariant equivalence classes of intrinsic states under propagation, yielding gauge structures isomorphic to Yang-Mills theory. The gauge group $SU(N)$ is uniquely determined by the number of stable normal modes supported by the medium. Third, through the same conventionalist measurement protocols, inhomogeneous distributions of the SM emerge isomorphically as curved spacetime geometry, governed by Einstein-type field equations from variational extremization of the medium's deformation free energy. This paper advances a unified interpretive account of the physical origin underlying the mathematical structures of both Relativity and the Standard Model.

</details>


### [133] [Holographic entanglement entropy with conformal boundary conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06277)
*Elena Cáceres,Hare Krishna,Harita Palani Balaji,Vaishnavi Patil*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有共形边界条件的三维AdS引力中的全息纠缠熵。通过扩展Lewkowycz-Maldacena-Dong的复制构造，推导出相应的全息纠缠熵公式，发现涨落的Weyl模不贡献额外熵。在全局AdS和BTZ几何中进行了显式计算，得到由有效中心荷c_eff支配的纠缠熵，并直接从对偶边界理论（全息CFT与类时Liouville理论耦合）验证了这一结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索具有共形边界条件的三维AdS引力中的全息纠缠熵，特别是理解Weyl模涨落对熵的贡献，以及K依赖的纠缠熵与有效中心荷c_eff之间的关系，并验证对偶边界理论的正确性。

Method: 扩展Lewkowycz-Maldacena-Dong的复制构造方法，推导具有固定共形类和外在曲率K、但Weyl模动力学化的共形边界条件下的全息纠缠熵公式；对全局AdS、旋转和非旋转BTZ几何进行显式计算；从对偶边界理论（holographic CFT + 类时Liouville理论 + T\bar{T}算符变形）直接计算纠缠熵进行验证。

Result: （1）涨落的Weyl模不贡献额外熵，全边界熵等于Bekenstein-Hawking熵，子区域熵服从Ryu-Takayanagi公式；（2）AdS_3中间隔的K依赖纠缠熵由c_m=3ℓ/2G_N控制；（3）高温共形温度下，熵由c_eff=(3ℓ/2G_N)(Kℓ-√(K²ℓ²-4))/2控制，与Cardy-like态密度中的有效中心荷一致；（4）从对偶边界理论得到的纠缠熵S_EE=(c_eff/3)ln(2R sinφ₀/ε)，为真空态提供了c_eff的独立边界实现，同时澄清该态并非全局AdS的对偶态。

Conclusion: 在具有共形边界条件的三维AdS引力中，纠缠熵仍由RT公式控制，Weyl模不贡献额外熵。K决定了有效中心荷c_eff，使得纠缠熵与Cardy公式中的有效中心荷一致。边界理论计算验证了这些结果，并澄清了真空态与全局AdS的对偶关系。

Abstract: We study holographic entanglement entropy in 3-dimensional AdS gravity with conformal boundary conditions which fix the conformal class of the boundary metric and its extrinsic curvature, $K$, while leaving the Weyl mode dynamical. Extending Lewkowycz-Maldacena-Dong's replica construction, we derive the corresponding holographic entanglement entropy formula. We show that the fluctuating Weyl mode does not contribute additional entropy. The entropy of the full boundary is therefore the Bekenstein-Hawking entropy, and the entropy of a boundary subregion continues to obey the Ryu-Takayanagi prescription, namely, the area of a minimal surface divided by $4G_N$.
  We carry out explicit calculations for global AdS, rotating and non-rotating BTZ geometries. For an interval in AdS$_3$ we find that the $K$-dependent holographic entanglement entropy is governed by $c_m=\frac{3 \ell}{2 G_N}.$ For a thermal state at high conformal temperatures we find that, the entropy is governed by $c_{\rm eff}=\frac{3 \ell}{2 G_N} \frac{K \ell- \sqrt{K^2 \ell^2-4}}{2}$, the same effective central charge that governs the Cardy-like density of states, in agreement with previous results in the literature.
  Finally, we also compute the entanglement entropy directly from the conjectured dual boundary theory - a holographic CFT coupled with time-like Liouville theory and deformed by a marginal $T \bar{T}$ like operator - and find $S_{EE} =\frac{c_{\rm eff}}{3} \ln\!\left(\frac{2 R \sinφ_0}ε\right) .$ This result for the state with no operator insertions (vacuum state ), provides an independent boundary realization of $c_{\mathrm{eff}}$ while clarifying that this state is not the state dual to global AdS.

</details>


### [134] [GLSM monodromy on quantum period lattice of Calabi-Yau fourfold flops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06280)
*Ban Lin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文针对某些Calabi-Yau四折叠（flop）上的B-brane中心荷建立了积分基，并利用梯度限制规则和窗口范畴精确推导了其单值（monodromy）表达式。单值被解释为与例外曲面收缩到锥奇点曲线相关的EZ twist，并进一步展示了EZ twist可分解为球面twist的实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Calabi-Yau四折叠flop上的B-brane单值结构，建立精确的积分基和monodromy表达式，深入探究EZ twist与球面twist之间的分解关系。

Method: 利用规范线性sigma模型（GLSM）的梯度限制规则和窗口范畴理论，建立B-brane中心荷的积分基；通过EZ twist解释monodromy，并在sixtic Calabi-Yau四折叠及格拉斯曼中的完全交上展示分解为球面twist。

Result: 成功建立了Calabi-Yau四折叠flop上B-brane中心荷的积分基，得到了精确的monodromy表达式，将其解释为EZ twist，并验证了在两类具体例子中EZ twist可分解为球面twist。

Conclusion: 该工作为Calabi-Yau四折叠flop上的B-brane单值结构提供了精确的数学描述，揭示了EZ twist与球面twist之间的内在联系，为理解高维Calabi-Yau流形的衍生范畴结构提供了新视角。

Abstract: We establish an integral basis for the B-brane central charges of certain Calabi-Yau fourfold flops and derive exact expressions for their monodromies using the grade restriction rule and window categories of the associated gauged linear sigma models. The monodromy is interpreted as an EZ twist associated with the contraction of an exceptional surface onto a curve of conifold singularities. We further illustrate a decomposition of the EZ twist into spherical twists for families of splitting configurations of the sixtic Calabi-Yau fourfold and a complete intersection in grassmannian.

</details>


### [135] [The Quantum Mechanics of Rare Events: From Quantum Walks to Stochastic Inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06319)
*Daniel Green,Kshitij Gupta,Akhil Premkumar*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文发展了量子系统大偏差理论的实用方面，用于计算从量子行走到宇宙学中各类物理系统的稀有涨落事件。作者首先分析非谐振荡器与热库耦合的案例，展示系统从统计涨落（热涨落）到量子涨落的演化过程，然后推广结果表明主导稀有涨落最小化测量诱导的相对熵，最后将该结果应用于宇宙学中随机暴胀产生的随机行走，发现固定德西特背景上长波长场密度矩阵的演化破坏KMS对称性，产生不满足细致平衡的稳态密度矩阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典统计系统中，大偏差原理已阐明半经典在稀有涨落描述中的作用，并为统计力学提供了数学基础。然而量子系统的大偏差理论发展尚不成熟。由于所有物理系统本质上都是量子力学的，这构成了理解统计物理、宇宙学等领域中稀有涨落的重要空白。本文旨在填补这一缺口，发展量子系统大偏差理论的实用工具。

Method: 论文首先分析非谐振荡器与热库耦合的具体案例，显式展示系统从统计涨落到量子涨落的演化；随后推广理论框架，证明主导稀有涨落最小化测量诱导的相对熵，从而为开放量子系统提供热力学描述；最后将结果应用于宇宙学随机暴胀中出现的量子行走，分析德西特背景上长波长场的密度矩阵演化。

Result: 研究发现，固定德西特背景上长波长场密度矩阵的演化破坏了KMS对称性，导致出现不满足细致平衡的稳态密度矩阵，这揭示了稀有涨落机制从经典统计行为到量子行为的过渡，并为开放量子系统的热力学描述提供了统一视角。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一套适用于量子系统的稀有涨落计算框架，通过最小化测量诱导相对熵来确定主导稀有事件路径，为理解从介观物理到宇宙学尺度的量子系统稀有涨落提供了统一的理论工具。

Abstract: Rare fluctuations in physical systems depend on the detailed microphysics responsible for the fluctuations. In classical statistical systems, the large deviation principle has elucidated the role of semi-classics in describing this regime, and has simultaneously provided a the mathematical foundation of statistical mechanics. Large deviation theory for quantum system is considerably less developed. As all physical systems are fundamentally quantum mechanical, this leaves a major gap in our understanding of rare fluctuations relevant to statistical physics, cosmology, and more. In this paper, we develop the practical aspects of the theory of large deviations relevant for calculating rare events in physical systems from quantum walks to cosmology. We first analyze the case of the anharmonic oscillator coupled to a bath, showing explicitly how the system evolves from dominantly statistical (e.g. thermal) to quantum fluctuations. We then generalize these results, showing that the dominant rare fluctuations minimize the measurement-induced relative entropy. This perspective provides a thermodynamic description of a wide range of open quantum systems. We apply these results to random walks that arise in cosmology through stochastic inflation. We show that the evolution of the density matrix of long wavelength fields on a fixed de Sitter background breaks the KMS symmetry, giving rise to a stationary density matrix that does not respect detailed balance.

</details>


### [136] [Quantum Lifts of Noninteger Power Law Field Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06282)
*Jarah Evslin,Hengyuan Guo,Stefano Bolognesi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出通过Hermite多项式展开并在质量尺度β下进行正规排序，为非整数幂次势能的场论提供量子提升方法。当α>2且β足够大时，真空态可在Fock态下微扰展开。将该方法应用于σ=4 Pöschl-Teller模型的φ^{5/2}势，证明β变形（量级为介子质量或更大）足以抑制kink模型中形状模的辐射激发发散，尽管激发概率仍受β依赖的反耦合分数幂增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非整数幂次势能的场论广泛应用于各种物理场景，但通常被认为只能作为有效模型而无法提升为真正的量子场论。特别地，σ=4 Pöschl-Teller模型的φ^{5/2}势在真空中三阶导数发散，导致三体相互作用发散；其kink的束缚形状模延伸至真空深处，辐射激发的概率看似发散。需要一种系统的方法来将这类模型量子化并处理这些发散。

Method: 将经典势能展开为Hermite多项式，然后在由参数β移动的质量尺度下进行正规排序（normal ordering），定义量子提升。通过选择足够大的β（当α>2时），使真空态能够在标准Fock态的基底下进行微扰展开。将该构造应用于σ=4 Pöschl-Teller模型，研究β变形对kink束缚模激发概率的影响。

Result: 当α>2时，只要β足够大，真空态可在普通Fock态下微扰展开。对于σ=4 Pöschl-Teller模型的φ^{5/2}势，量级为介子质量或更大的β变形足以消除三点相互作用和束缚模激发概率的发散。然而，激发概率仍被增强，增强因子为β依赖的反耦合常数的分数幂。

Conclusion: 通过Hermite展开与β尺度正规排序的方法，可为非整数幂势能的场论提供一致的量子提升，使原本看似发散的物理量（如Pöschl-Teller模型中的三点相互作用和kink形状模激发概率）变得有限，同时揭示了残余的β依赖的分数幂增强效应，为这类有效模型的量子化提供了新的框架。

Abstract: Field theories whose potentials have noninteger power laws $α$ have found many applications, but are often claimed to have no lift to quantum field theory except as effective models. We define quantum lifts by expanding the classical potential in Hermite polynomials and then normal ordering at a mass scale shifted by a parameter $β$. We find that when $α>2$, for sufficiently large $β$, the vacuum state can be perturbatively expanded in usual Fock states. We apply this to the following problem. The $σ=4$ Pöschl-Teller model has a $φ^{5/2}$ potential. As the third derivative of the potential diverges in each vacuum, one expects the three point interactions to diverge in the vacuum. The model's kink has three shape modes and the least bound mode extends so far into the vacuum that its probability of being excited by radiation apparently diverges. We show that a deformation $β$ of order the meson mass or larger is sufficient to tame this divergence, although it nonetheless results in an excitation probability which is enhanced by a $β$-dependent fractional power of the inverse coupling.

</details>


### [137] [A Tale of Two Compact Bosons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06376)
*Christian Ferko,Vishnu Jejjala,Brandon Robinson*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文综述了神经网络场论（NN-FT）中混合连续/离散潜变量构造的框架，并将其应用于两类紧致玻色子系统：BKT相变和玻色弦理论。研究表明，相同的局部神经采样器配合不同的离散拓扑数据（涡旋扇区或动量-绕数标记），可以重现BKT临界线、涡旋增殖、关联长度奇异行为及Nelson-Kosterlitz跳变，以及T对偶、Buscher变换、自对偶流代数增强等弦论特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络场论在紧致理论中仅处理局部高斯扇区是不充分的，必须考虑离散拓扑扇区的求和。作者旨在建立统一的混合连续/离散潜变量框架，证明同一局部神经采样器配合不同离散拓扑数据可生成物理上不同的紧致理论。

Method: 采用混合连续/离散潜变量构造：对于BKT相变，使用随机傅里叶特征自旋波采样器配合显式库仑气体涡旋扇区；对于玻色弦，使用振子模配以动量-绕数标记，并结合紧致环面上的Buscher变换和T对偶结构。

Result: 该方法成功再现了BKT相变中Tc以下的Gaussian临界线、Tc以上的涡旋增殖、关联长度的本征奇异性以及Nelson-Kosterlitz跳变；在玻色弦方面复现了圆环T对偶、常环流背景上的Buscher变换、自对偶流代数增强以及玩具T-fold结构。

Conclusion: 核心结论是：局部神经采样器本身不决定理论的物理性质，离散拓扑数据的选取才是区分不同紧致理论的关键。这一框架为利用神经网络构建和探索具有非平凡拓扑结构的场论提供了系统化方法。

Abstract: Neural network field theory (NN-FT) defines a field theory by a network architecture together with a probability density on its latent variables. For compact theories the local Gaussian sector is only part of the story: one must also sum over discrete topological sectors. We review a mixed continuous/discrete latent-variable construction and apply it to two compact bosons. For the Berezinskii--Kosterlitz--Thouless transition, a random Fourier feature spin-wave sampler supplemented by an explicit Coulomb gas vortex sector reproduces the Gaussian critical line below $T_c$, vortex proliferation above $T_c$, the essential singularity of the correlation length, and the Nelson--Kosterlitz jump. For the bosonic string, oscillator modes augmented by momentum--winding labels reproduce circle T-duality, Buscher transformations on constant toroidal backgrounds, self-dual current algebra enhancement, and a toy T-fold. The common lesson is that the same local neural sampler, paired with different discrete topological data, yields physically distinct compact theories. These proceedings are based on arXiv:2604.02313.

</details>

### [138] [On the motivic cohomology of some singular rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05220)
*Tess Bouis*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该论文利用非 A¹-不变动机上同调，通过积分 p-adic Hodge 理论替代标准的迹方法，改进了若干奇异环（如 Z/p^n、Z[x]/(x^e) 以及紧 Hausdorff 空间 X 上的连续函数环 C(X;C)）代数 K-理论的已知计算结果，给出了动机层面的精化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的代数 K-理论计算（尤其是对奇异环）通常依赖于迹方法（trace methods），但这种方法有其局限性。作者希望利用非 A¹-不变的动机上同调这一更精细的工具，配合积分 p-adic Hodge 理论，为已知的 K-理论结果提供动机层面的精化，从而揭示更深层的结构信息。

Method: 作者采用非 A¹-不变的动机上同调作为主要工具，替代标准的 A¹-不变动机上同调框架。关键创新在于使用积分 p-adic Hodge 理论（integral p-adic Hodge theory）来取代传统 K-理论计算中使用的迹方法，这使得对 Z/p^n、Z[x]/(x^e) 及紧 Hausdorff 空间的连续函数环等奇异环的 K-理论计算能够在动机层面对已知结果进行精化。

Result: 成功地证明了上述若干奇异环的代数 K-理论的动机精化版本，包括环 Z/p^n、Z[x]/(x^e) 以及紧 Hausdorff 空间上的连续函数环 C(X;C) 的情形。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了非 A¹-不变的动机上同调与积分 p-adic Hodge 理论的结合，可以作为一种强有力的替代方案来精化奇异环的代数 K-理论计算，为迹方法之外提供了新的计算范式。

Abstract: Using non-$\mathbb{A}^1$-invariant motivic cohomology, we prove motivic refinements of certain known computations of the algebraic $K$-theory of singular rings, such as rings of the form $\mathbb{Z}/p^n$, $\mathbb{Z}[x]/(x^e)$, and $\mathscr{C}(X;\mathbb{C})$ for $X$ a compact Hausdorff space. These refinements are made possible by the use of integral $p$-adic Hodge theory, as a replacement for the standard use of trace methods in $K$-theory.

</details>


### [139] [Elliptic matroids and modular curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05299)
*Matthew Baker*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文为秩3拟阵T_n（其基为Z/nZ上非零和三元子集）与模曲线X_1(n)^\circ之间建立了自然双射，并将此对应提升为Z[1/n]上的概形同构；该结果将模曲线与拟阵实现空间联系起来，并以拟阵的不可表示性等价于Mazur定理的素数阶情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在为Borisov和Roulleau关于X_1(n)与拟阵T_n的复几何对应提供纯代数和关联理论的证明，并将其推广到任意域和算术层面，从而揭示模曲线理论与拟阵理论之间更深层的结构联系。

Method: (1) 采用纯代数与关联理论（incidence-theoretic）方法证明对任意域k（char(k)∤n）存在自然双射X_1(n)^\circ(k)与T_n的k-实现类的重定标类之间的对应；(2) 利用形变理论（deformation-theoretic）论证来验证Artin局部环值点上的同构，从而将域值对应升级为Z[1/n]上的概形同构；(3) 在附录中，利用Baker-Jin-Lorscheid的带概形理论，将拟阵实现空间从Z上的仿射概形提升为F_1^{\pm}上的仿射带概形。

Result: 对n≥10，给出纯代数且关联理论式的证明，表明对任意char(k)不整除n的域k，X_1(n)^\circ(k)与T_n的k-实现类的重定标类之间存在自然双射；将此对应升级为Z[1/n]上的概形同构，从而X_1(n)^\circ在Z[1/n]上获得拟阵实现空间的自然模型。对素数p≥11，T_p在Q上的不可表示性等价于Mazur关于椭圆曲线有理扭转点的著名定理的素数阶情形。

Conclusion: 该工作建立并推广了模曲线X_1(n)^\circ与拟阵T_n之间的对应关系，不仅恢复了Borisov-Roulleau的复几何结果，还将其提升为全局算术范畴的概形同构，并通过拟阵实现空间的视角为Mazur定理提供了一个新的等价表述。

Abstract: For $n\geq 4$, let $T_n$ be the rank-3 matroid on $\mathbb{Z}/n\mathbb{Z}$ whose bases are the three-element non-zero-sum subsets. Let $X_1(n)^\circ$ denote the open subscheme of the modular curve $X_1(n)$ obtained by removing the cusps corresponding to reducible Néron polygons. For $n \geq 10$, we give a purely algebraic and incidence-theoretic proof that, for every field $k$ with $\mathrm{char}(k)$ not dividing $n$, there is a natural bijection between $X_1(n)^\circ(k)$ and rescaling classes of $k$-realizations of $T_n$. For $k = \mathbb{C}$, this recovers a theorem of Borisov and Roulleau.
  We then upgrade the field-valued correspondence to an isomorphism of schemes over $\mathbb{Z}[1/n]$. The main new ingredient is a deformation-theoretic argument which allows us to verify the isomorphism on points valued in Artinian local rings. As consequences, the modular curve $X_1(n)^\circ$ acquires a natural model over $\mathbb{Z}[1/n]$ as a matroid realization space, and, for primes $p \geq 11$, the non-representability of $T_p$ over $\mathbb{Q}$ is equivalent to the prime-order case of Mazur's celebrated theorem on rational torsion points of elliptic curves.
  In an appendix, we explain how to upgrade the realization space of a matroid from an affine scheme over $\mathbb{Z}$ to an affine band scheme (in the sense of Baker-Jin-Lorscheid) over $\mathbb{F}_1^{\pm}$.

</details>


### [140] [The weak Markus--Yamabe conjecture fails in dimension 14](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05392)
*Álvaro Castañeda,Gerardo Honorato,Francisco Valenzuela-Henríquez*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了弱Markus–Yamabe猜想在维数n≥14时均不成立。作者首先建立了一个“链实现定理”：任意多项式Keller映射F=I+H（分量次数为d_i）可构造一个显式多项式向量场，其雅可比矩阵处处具有谱{-1}，且其奇点与F的任意给定纤维双射对应。将此定理应用于Jacobian猜想的最新反例，得到R^14上次数为7、具有三个有理奇点的Hurwitz向量场；通过对相关三次镇定映射的降秩去齐次化，独立地得到R^18上次数为3的Hurwitz向量场。维数3≤n≤13的情形仍然开放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱Markus–Yamabe猜想是微分方程和动力系统领域的一个长期未解问题，它断言：若一个多项式向量场的雅可比矩阵处处谱位于左半平面，则其所有轨道收敛到单个奇点。此前仅在低维情形部分验证，高维是否成立长期未知。本文旨在通过将Jacobian猜想（多项式映射的反函数问题）与Hurwitz向量场（雅可比谱为{-1}的向量场）之间建立联系，利用Jacobian猜想的最新反例来构造反例，从而否定该猜想在高维的成立。

Method: 方法分为三个步骤：(1) 证明链实现定理：对任意多项式Keller映射F=I+H（n维，分量次数d_1,...,d_n），构造一个N维（N=∑max(d_i,2)-n）的显式多项式向量场，使其雅可比矩阵处处谱为{-1}，且奇点与F的任意给定纤维一一对应；(2) 将该定理应用于Jacobian猜想的最新反例（一个满足Keller条件的非常值多项式映射），从而得到R^14上次数为7、具有三个有理奇点的Hurwitz向量场；(3) 通过对相关的三次镇定映射进行秩约减的去齐次化处理，独立地构造出R^18上次数为3的Hurwitz向量场。

Result: 本文取得以下主要结果：在R^14上构造了一个次数为7的Hurwitz向量场，具有三个有理奇点，这直接否定了弱Markus–Yamabe猜想在维数14的情形；同时通过在R^18上构造一个次数为3的Hurwitz向量场作为独立验证。由此确立了该猜想在一切n≥14维均不成立。维数3≤n≤13的情形仍然开放。

Conclusion: 弱Markus–Yamabe猜想在所有维数n≥14上被推翻，但3≤n≤13维的情形仍未解决。本文的创新贡献在于建立了Jacobian猜想与弱Markus–Yamabe猜想之间的桥梁——链实现定理，使得Jacobian猜想的最新反例可以系统地转化为反例向量场。此外，R^18上的三次Hurwitz向量场以较低的次数独立验证了结论，表明这一联系具有更强的普适性。维数3至13的开放问题提示可能存在维数依赖的临界阈值。

Abstract: We show that the weak Markus--Yamabe conjecture fails in every dimension $n\geq14$. We first prove a chain realization theorem: every polynomial Keller map $F=\I+H$ of $\R^n$ with component degrees $d_1,\ldots,d_n$ yields an explicit polynomial vector field on $\R^N$, with $N=\sum_i\max(d_i,2)-n$, whose Jacobian matrix has spectrum $\{-1\}$ at every point and whose singularities are in bijection with any prescribed fiber of $F$. Applied to the recent counterexample to the Jacobian conjecture, this gives a Hurwitz vector field of degree seven on $\R^{14}$ with three rational singularities. A rank-reduced dehomogenization of the associated cubic stabilization gives, independently, a Hurwitz field of degree three on $\R^{18}$. The dimensions $3\leq n\leq13$ remain open.

</details>


### [141] [Elliptic curve counting in toric threefolds: virtual, enumerative, and tropical](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05486)
*Sae Koyama*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究三维环簇中椭圆曲线的枚举几何，引入"良间距计数"（well-spaced counts）这类枚举整不变量，通过在R³中的良间距亏格一热带曲线进行刻画，并与对数退化公式比较，建立了对数虚拟不变量与几何不变量之间的显式关系，获得了Getzler-Pandharipande关于P³中椭圆曲线公式的对数类比。应用上证明P³关于环边界的虚拟对数不变量在次数足够大时严格小于普通Gromov-Witten不变量，并给出若干示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究三维环簇中椭圆曲线的枚举几何，特别是在对数Gromov-Witten理论与经典枚举计数之间建立联系。作者旨在将对数虚拟不变量通过可计算的几何不变量（良间距计数）加以刻画，并探索对数不变量与普通Gromov-Witten不变量之间的定量关系。

Method: 利用R³中良间距亏格一热带曲线的枚举计数（良间距计数）作为工具，结合对数退化公式（logarithmic degeneration formula），将对数虚拟不变量与这些几何整数不变量建立显式关系，类似于Getzler-Pandharipande在P³中椭圆曲线的经典公式。通过比较两类不变量，推导出对数情况下不等式的证明。

Result: 获得了对数模下Getzler-Pandharipande公式的类比结果：对数虚拟不变量可由良间距计数显式表达。作为应用，证明了P³关于其环边界的虚拟对数不变量在次数足够大时严格小于普通Gromov-Witten不变量，并提供了具体示例加以验证。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了对数Gromov-Witten理论中椭圆曲线不变量与经典枚举几何之间的桥梁，给出了对数类比公式，揭示了在环边界的对数虚拟不变量与普通Gromov-Witten不变量之间的严格不等式关系，丰富了退化方法在枚举几何中的应用。

Abstract: We study the enumerative geometry of elliptic curves in toric threefolds. We consider enumerative integer invariants, called well-spaced counts, which can be studied using well-spaced genus-one tropical curves in $\mathbb{R}^3$. By comparing this with the logarithmic degeneration formula, we obtain an explicit relationship between logarithmic virtual invariants and these geometric invariants. The result is a logarithmic analogue of a formula of Getzler--Pandharipande for elliptic curves in $\mathbb{P}^3$. As an application, we show that the virtual logarithmic invariants for $\mathbb{P}^3$ with respect to its toric boundary are strictly less than the ordinary Gromov--Witten invariants once the degree is sufficiently large. Several examples are included.

</details>


### [142] [On the Numerical Terao Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05662)
*Piotr Pokora*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了偶次数、仅含ADE奇点的圆锥-直线排列满足数值寺尾猜想（Numerical Terao Conjecture），但构造了次数为9的反例（七条直线加一条光滑圆锥，仅含普通四重点），表明该猜想在更广泛的拟齐次设定下不成立，并给出最强的已知反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值寺尾猜想是超平面排列理论中一个核心未解猜想，它将排列的拓扑组合信息与代数不变量（自由性）联系起来。此前该猜想在低维或特定类别中已有部分验证，但尚未在圆锥-直线排列这一更广的几何类别中充分研究。本文旨在探索该猜想在具有奇异性的圆锥-直线排列中的有效性边界，并寻找可能的反例。

Method: 作者首先利用ADE奇点的代数几何性质（特别是加藤-斋藤理论或相关的Tjurina数计算）证明偶次数且仅含ADE奇点的圆锥-直线排列满足数值寺尾猜想。随后，在拟齐次设定下，通过构造两个次数为9的排列（均由七条直线和一条光滑圆锥组成），它们具有相同的弱组合结构W(CL)=(7,1; 8A1+D4+4X9)，但一个排列是自由的（指数为(4,4)），另一个是近自由的（指数为(3,6)），从而产生反例。

Result: 主要结果包括两个部分：其一，偶次数、仅含ADE奇点的圆锥-直线排列满足数值寺尾猜想；其二，在拟齐次设定下引入普通四重点后，构造出次数为9的反例，其中一对同弱组合的排列分别具有不同的自由指数(4,4)和(3,6)，这推翻了数值寺尾猜想最强形式的表述。

Conclusion: 该研究精确划分了数值寺尾猜想的有效范围：在ADE奇点约束下猜想成立，但在允许普通四重点的拟齐次情形下猜想失败。这一反例表明，组合信息W(CL)不足以唯一确定排列的自由性，对数值寺尾猜想的强形式提出了否定性结论。

Abstract: We prove that the Numerical Terao Conjecture holds for even-degree conic-line arrangements having only ADE singularities. We then show that the conjecture fails in the broader quasi-homogeneous setting once ordinary quadruple points are admitted, by constructing a degree-nine counterexample, each consisting of seven lines and one smooth conic, with the same weak combinatorics \[ W(\mathcal{CL}) = (7,1;\,8A_1+D_4+4X_9). \] We show that one curve is free with exponents $(4,4)$, whereas the other is nearly free with exponents $(3,6)$. This yields a counterexample to the strongest known formulation of the Numerical Terao Conjecture.

</details>


### [143] [Real Structures in the Moduli of Projective Models of K3-Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05941)
*Çisem Güneş Aktaş*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究具有指定简单奇点的K3曲面复族中是否包含实代数曲面，开发了一种显式且统一的算法，用于检测任何等奇异层中的实代表。作为主要应用，解决了空间四次曲面的问题，证明除三种例外类型外，所有实等奇异层都包含实曲面，并恢复了平面六次曲线中唯一已知的没有实代表的简单六次曲线的实层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究K3曲面的实表示问题：给定具有特定简单奇异点的复K3曲面族，需要判断其中是否存在实代数曲面。此前该问题缺乏统一且显式的判定算法，尤其是对所有极化和任意等奇异层的系统性研究尚未完成。

Method: 开发了一种显式且统一的算法，用于在任何等奇异层中检测实代表，适用于所有极化情况。该方法基于对K3曲面复形变族的系统分析，通过建构算法来确定在给定等奇异层内是否存在实的成员。

Result: 作为主要应用，完全解决了空间四次曲面（spatial quartics）的实可表示性问题：除三种例外类型外，所有实等奇异层都包含实曲面。此外，该方法重新审视并恢复了平面六次曲线中唯一已知的、没有实代表的简单六次曲线的实层。

Conclusion: 建立了K3曲面等奇异层中实代表判定的统一算法框架，完整解决了空间四次曲面的情况，并验证了方法在平面六次曲线上的适用性，说明该算法具有普适性。

Abstract: This paper investigates when a complex family of K3-surfaces with prescribed simple singularities contains real algebraic surfaces. We develop an explicit and uniform algorithm for detecting real representatives in any equisingular stratum, valid for all polarizations. As a primary application, we resolve the problem for spatial quartics, showing that all real equisingular strata contain real surfaces except for three exceptional types, thereby completing the study of real representability in this case. Our method also revisits the analogous phenomenon in the realm of plane sextics, recovering the unique known real stratum of simple sextics without real representatives.

</details>


### [144] [About finite differential tropical basis for linear ODE's](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06018)
*Stefano Mereta,Alejandro Vargas*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文探讨线性常微分方程的热带化问题，旨在开发计算经典解收敛半径的方法，并通过引入有限微分热带基（Fink和Toghani, 2022提出）的概念来表征方程的类别，初步探索聚焦于二阶和三阶情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主要动机是开发计算线性ODE经典解收敛半径的方法。为此，关键在于刻画哪些类别的方程承认有限微分热带基，这是实现该目标的首要步骤。

Method: 以有限微分热带基（由Fink和Toghani在2022年引入）为核心工具，通过对二阶和三阶线性ODE的初步探索，系统性地提出并研究若干关于线性ODE热带化的开放问题。

Result: 论文目前处于问题提出与初步探索阶段，尚未给出完整结果；初步探索覆盖了二阶和三阶情形，为后续建立完整的有限微分热带基理论奠定基础。

Conclusion: 本文通过公式化若干开放问题，明确了热带化线性ODE的研究方向和关键难点，指出刻画承认有限微分热带基的方程类别是核心目标，二阶和三阶情形的探索为更一般情形的研究提供了起点。

Abstract: We formulate several open questions regarding the tropicalization of linear ODEs, aiming primarily to develop methods for calculating the radius of convergence of their classical solutions. To this aim it is of foremost importance to characterize the classes of equations that admit a finite differential tropical basis, as introduced in (Fink and Toghani, 2022). Our initial exploration examines the second- and third-order cases.

</details>


### [145] [Fundamental groups of the complements to reducible curves on smooth surfaces with restricted classes of components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06136)
*A. Libgober*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文对光滑单连通射影曲面上对偶簇次数不超过6的曲线铅笔等奇异性族进行了分类，并据此描述了满足特定条件且基本群存在到秩大于6自由群的本质满射的曲线补空间的基本群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究光滑单连通射影曲面上曲线铅笔的等奇异性分类是一个核心几何问题。通过对偶簇的次数限制（≤6）以及曲线分量条件的约束，可以对这些族进行完整分类，进而深入理解补空间基本群的结构。

Method: 本文采用分类方法，对满足对偶簇次数不超过6且包含足够多分量满足特定超平面截面条件的曲线的铅笔族进行分类研究，并利用此分类结果来分析补空间基本群到自由群的满射性质。

Result: 成功对所有满足上述条件的等奇异性曲线铅笔族进行了分类，并推导出满足条件的曲线补空间基本群的结构描述——即当存在到秩大于6自由群的本质满射时，其基本群的具体形态。

Conclusion: 该分类结果与基本群描述共同揭示了在这类曲面上的曲线补空间基本群在特定自由度条件下的限制性结构，深化了对射影曲面曲线布局与基本群之间关系的理解。

Abstract: We classify equisingular families of pencils of curves on smooth simply connected projective surfaces whose dual varieties have degree at most 6, and which contain sufficiently large number of members which irreducible components are either hyperplane sections or irreducible components of hyperplane sections. As a consequence, we describe the fundamental groups of complements to curves on such surfaces whose irreducible components satisfy the above condition and whose fundamental groups admit an essential surjection onto a free group of rank greater than 6.

</details>


### [146] [Compactification Independence of the Irregular Hodge Filtration on Deligne-Mumford Stacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06234)
*Haoxu Wang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了对带正则函数的平滑分离Deligne-Mumford栈，由紧化诱导的Yu滤过和Kontsevich格不依赖于紧化的选择。证明通过好的消解和栈弱分解，将比较归结为沿边界除子的膨胀和根变换。该结论按扇区应用于Harder-Lee的栈Clarke镜像配对，证明轨形滤过和不规则Hodge数仅依赖于栈Landau-Ginzburg模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在研究Deligne-Mumford栈上的Landau-Ginzburg模型的twisted de Rham上同调时，Yu定义了依赖于紧化的滤过，Kontsevich格也由同一紧化给出。然而紧化中的函数延拓可能仅是有理函数，且在极除子附近仅满足局部非退化条件，因此需要证明这种构造不依赖于紧化的选择，以保证几何不变量是良定义的。

Method: 首先利用好的消解（good resolutions）和栈弱分解（stacky weak factorization）定理，将任意两个紧化之间的比较约化为沿边界除子的膨胀（blowups）和根变换（root constructions）这两种基本操作。然后分别对这两种操作建立Yu复形和Kontsevich复形的滤过比较定理，从而逐步实现滤过不依赖于紧化的证明。

Result: 证明了由紧化定义的Yu滤过不依赖于紧化的选择，并且Kontsevich格计算该滤过。该独立性按扇区（sector by sector）应用到Harder-Lee的stacky Clarke镜像配对中，得到轨形不规则Hodge数仅依赖于栈Landau-Ginzburg模型。

Conclusion: Yu滤过和Kontsevich格对于栈Landau-Ginzburg模型是内在的几何不变量，不依赖于用于定义的紧化选择。这为stacky Clarke镜像对称性研究中的轨形不规则Hodge数提供了坚实的不变性基础。

Abstract: Let $(\mathscr U,w)$ be a smooth separated Deligne--Mumford stack of finite type over $\mathbb C$, with a regular function $w$. Following Yu, one can use a compactification to define a filtration on its twisted de Rham cohomology. The same compactification gives Kontsevich lattices that compute this filtration. The extension of $w$ on the compactification may be only rational. Only a local nondegeneracy condition near the polar divisor is imposed.
  We prove that the resulting filtration does not depend on the compactification. The proof uses good resolutions and stacky weak factorization. This reduces the comparison to blowups and roots along boundary divisors. Filtered comparison theorems for the Yu and Kontsevich complexes are established for both operations.
  Harder and Lee use orbifold irregular Hodge numbers in their study of stacky Clarke mirror pairs. Compactification independence applies sector by sector to their setting. Thus the resulting orbifold filtration and irregular Hodge numbers depend only on the stack Landau--Ginzburg model.

</details>


### [147] [Frobenius-orbit slicing and uniform elimination of positive-dimensional singular loci](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06273)
*Yutong Zhang,Yaoran Yang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究射影空间上的固定整拟射影子簇X（在Z上光滑，相对维数r）上，均匀d次形式的限制的第m阶主部射流具有正维零点概型的概率，给出上界C(d+1)^{N_m}p^{-λ_m(d)}。当m=1时得到Bertini奇异轨迹估计，解决Poonen的算术Bertini猜想5.2，并可通过提高次数阈值获得任意固定A>0的p^{-A}界。对c个独立超曲面，Jacobian秩退化轨迹的概率也有相应上界。证明采用过滤Q-adic分解、三角正规Taylor块和Jacobian主元图等技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该论文旨在解决算术几何中著名的Poonen算术Bertini猜想，该猜想关注随机多项式的限制在算术子簇上产生奇异性的概率。此前的研究仅对m=1的情形给出估计，本文推广到任意阶射流（m≥1），并解决多个独立超曲面情形下的Jacobian秩退化问题，为算术Bertini定理提供完整的概率上界。

Method: 论文采用三种关键技术：过滤Q-adic分解（filtered Q-adic decompositions）来精细控制射流空间的结构；三角正规Taylor块（triangular normal Taylor blocks）用于规范多项式的局部行为；Jacobian主元图（Jacobian-pivot charts）用于控制Jacobian矩阵退化位置的复杂度。这些工具共同实现了对射流零点概型正维度概率的统一上界估计。

Result: 对固定m≥1，得到概率上界C(d+1)^{N_m}p^{-λ_m(d)}，其中N_m=binom(r+m,m)，λ_m(d)=floor(m(d+1)/(m+1))。m=1时给出Bertini奇异轨迹估计C(d+1)^{r+1}p^{-ceil(d/2)}，解决了Poonen的算术Bertini猜想5.2。增加次数阈值后，对任意A>0均可得p^{-A}界。对c≤r个独立超曲面，Jacobian秩退化概率同时受C∑(d_i+1)^{r+1}p^{-ceil(d_i/2)}和C'(d_min+1)^{r+1}p^{-ceil(d_min/2)}两个上界约束。

Conclusion: 本文完整解决了Poonen的算术Bertini猜想5.2，并将结果从一阶射流推广到任意m阶射流，同时处理了多个独立超曲面的情形。所得概率界在提高次数阈值后可达到任意多项式衰减率p^{-A}，为算术统计几何提供了强有力的工具。

Abstract: Let $\cX\subseteq\Pj^n_{\Z}$ be a fixed integral quasiprojective subscheme, smooth over $\Z$ of relative dimension $r$. For each fixed $m\ge1$, we bound the probability that the $m$th principal-parts jet of the restriction of a uniform degree-$d$ form to $\cX_p$ has a positive-dimensional zero scheme. The bound is $C(d+1)^{N_m}p^{-λ_m(d)}$, where $N_m=\binom{r+m}{m}$ and $λ_m(d)=\floor{m(d+1)/(m+1)}$. For $m=1$, this gives the Bertini singular-locus estimate $C(d+1)^{r+1}p^{-\ceil{d/2}}$. It settles Poonen's arithmetic Bertini Conjecture~5.2 and, after increasing the degree threshold, yields $p^{-A}$ for every fixed $A>0$. For $c\le r$ independent hypersurfaces, the probability of a positive-dimensional Jacobian rank-degeneracy locus is bounded both by $C\sum_i(d_i+1)^{r+1}p^{-\ceil{d_i/2}}$ and by $C'(d_{\min}+1)^{r+1}p^{-\ceil{d_{\min}/2}}$. The proof uses filtered $Q$-adic decompositions, triangular normal Taylor blocks, and Jacobian-pivot charts of uniformly controlled complexity.

</details>


### [148] [The graded Betti numbers are not tropical invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06284)
*Noah Laikin*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了分级Betti数不是齐次理想热带化或值域上射影代数簇热带化的不变量，通过构造一族饱和理想实现相同的热带理想但其中一个成员的Betti表与其他不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究热带化是否保留了代数几何中的某些数值不变量，特别是判断Betti数（衡量理想解析层的最小自由分解结构）是否在热带化过程中保持不变。

Method: 构造一族饱和理想，使其全部实现相同的热带理想（即它们的热带化相同），但比较它们的分级Betti表以找出其中与众不同的成员。

Result: 找到了一族饱和理想，它们共享同一个热带理想，但其中一个成员的Betti表与其他成员不同，从而证明Betti数不是热带化的不变量。

Conclusion: 分级Betti数不能作为热带化意义下的不变量来使用，热带化丢失了关于最小免费分解的某些精细结构信息，这对热带几何与交换代数交叉领域有重要影响。

Abstract: We show that the graded Betti numbers are not invariants of the tropicalisation of a homogeneous ideal, or the tropicalisation of a projective algebraic scheme over a valued field. We do this by producing a family of saturated ideals that all realise the same tropical ideal with one member having a distinct Betti table from the rest.

</details>


### [149] [Stability of kernel bundles on projective bundles over curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06365)
*Abel Castorena,George H. Hitching*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明：当曲线C、射影丛X及其生成子空间V在模空间中一般，且相对超平面丛OX(1)充分丰富时，系统(OX(1),V)的核丛相对于OX(1)是稳定的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究射影丛上线性系统的核丛稳定性，这是代数几何中一个基础且重要的问题。核丛的稳定性与系统的几何性质密切相关，作者希望在更一般的背景下（射影丛而非射影空间）给出稳定性的充分条件。

Method: 在光滑亏格g≥3的曲线上取射影丛X，考虑其相对超平面丛OX(1)及其生成子空间V。通过取"一般模"的条件（C、X、V在相应模空间中为一般点），并假设OX(1)充分丰富，利用模空间的一般性质来证明核丛的OX(1)-稳定性。

Result: 证实了在C、X和V一般（模意义下）且OX(1)充分丰富的条件下，系统(OX(1),V)的核丛是OX(1)-稳定的。

Conclusion: 该结果将核丛稳定性从射影空间推广到了更一般的射影丛情形，为理解高维代数簇上线性系统核丛的稳定性提供了新的理论依据。

Abstract: Let $X$ be a projective bundle over a smooth curve $C$ of genus $g \ge 3$, and consider the relative hyperplane bundle ${\mathcal O}_X (1) \to X$. Let $V \subseteq H^0 ( X , {\mathcal O}_X (1) )$ be a generating subspace. We prove that when $C$, $X$ and $V$ are general in moduli and ${\mathcal O}_X (1)$ is sufficiently ample, the kernel bundle of the system $({\mathcal O}_X (1) , V)$ is ${\mathcal O}_X (1)$-stable.

</details>
